### SeedListMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Seed List Generator (C2C)

Generate N deterministic seeds from a base seed. Modes: increment (base, base+1, …), hash (sha256 of base+index, useful for de-correlated samples), random (mt19937 stream). Optional per-shot seed maps and a reproducibility manifest.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/helpers/helpers.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `base_seed` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…4294967295 | The seed every other seed is derived from. |
| `count` | `INT` | default `4`, range 1…1024 | How many seeds to make. |
| `mode` | choice: `increment`, `hash`, `random` | default `"increment"` | increment: base, base+1, ...; hash: sha256 of base and index (unrelated seeds); random: a seeded random stream. All are repeatable for the same base. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `shot_ids` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Optional shot ids (comma or one per line, e.g. 'sh010, sh020'). Each shot gets its own 'count' seeds (one per frame index) in seed_map_json, made with the same mode from a base derived from base_seed and the shot id. Empty: no map. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `first_seed` | `INT` | The first seed of the list. |
| 1 | `csv_all_seeds` | `STRING` | Every seed, comma separated (e.g. for a seed sweep). |
| 2 | `seeds` | `INT` | Every seed as a list: a node wired here runs once per seed. |
| 3 | `seed_map_json` | `STRING` | Per-shot seed map as JSON {shot id: [seed of frame 0, frame 1, ...]} (count seeds per shot). A shot's seeds depend only on base_seed and the shot's id, so reordering or adding shots never changes them. '{}' when shot_ids is empty. |
| 4 | `manifest_json` | `STRING` | Reproducibility manifest (JSON): the recipe (base seed, count, mode, algorithm, shot ids), every seed and seed map, and run metadata (prompt fingerprint, node id, Python / torch / ComfyUI versions, UTC time). |


---

### TextTemplateMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Text Template (C2C)

Substitute {a}{b}{c}{d} placeholders in a template string. Useful for building prompts from per-shot variables. Optional: more named variables, a typed schema (defaults, required fields, validation) and an extended engine with {?name}...{/name} sections and {name|upper} filters - plain text substitution, no code is ever run.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/helpers/helpers.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `template` | `STRING` | default `"a {a} of {b}, {c}, {d}"`, multiline | Text with {a} {b} {c} {d} placeholders; each is replaced by the input of that name. |
| `a` | `STRING` | default `""` | Replaces {a} in the template. |
| `b` | `STRING` | default `""` | Replaces {b} in the template. |
| `c` | `STRING` | default `""` | Replaces {c} in the template. |
| `d` | `STRING` | default `""` | Replaces {d} in the template. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `variables` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | More named variables, one 'name = value' per line (e.g. 'style = anime'); use them as {style}. They may also override a-d. '#' lines are comments. |
| `schema` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Optional typed schema, one per line: 'name: text\|int\|float\|bool\|choice(x\|y) [= default] [required]', e.g. 'steps: int = 20' or 'style: choice(photo\|anime) required'. Empty values take the default; a missing required value or a wrong type stops the run with a list of the problems. |
| `engine` | choice: `format`, `extended` | default `"format"` | format: Python-style {name} substitution as before (unknown or broken braces are left as they are). extended: also {?name}...{/name} (kept when name is set and not false/0/no/off), {?name=value}...{/name}, {!name}...{/name} (kept when empty), filters {name\|upper\|lower\|title\|capitalize\|strip\|default:x\|prefix:x\|suffix:x\|truncate:N}; '{{' '}}' are literal braces; an unknown name is an error. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `text` | `STRING` | The template with its placeholders filled in. |
| 1 | `report` | `STRING` | JSON: the engine, every variable's final value (after schema defaults and type checks) and, for the extended engine, which conditional sections were kept or dropped. |


---

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Inpaint


### InpaintCropProMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Inpaint Crop Pro

Crop around mask for inpainting (lquesada API + Wan 2.2 Animate aware). crop_mode 'smart aspect' is the stills crop sized between a min and a max side; 'mask prepare' cleans the mask without cropping. Pair with Inpaint Stitch Pro (C2C).

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/inpaint_suite.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `downscale_algorithm` | choice: `nearest`, `bilinear`, `bicubic`, `lanczos`, `box`, `hamming`, `area` | default `"bilinear"` | Resampling used when something is made smaller (pre-resize, fitting the crop to the target). area / box keep detail best when shrinking. |
| `upscale_algorithm` | choice: `nearest`, `bilinear`, `bicubic`, `lanczos`, `box`, `hamming`, `area` | default `"bicubic"` | Resampling used when something is made bigger. bicubic / lanczos are sharp; nearest keeps hard edges. |
| `preresize` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Resize input image before processing (lquesada-style). |
| `preresize_mode` | choice: `ensure minimum resolution`, `ensure maximum resolution`, `ensure minimum and maximum resolution` | default `"ensure minimum resolution"` | preresize: scale the whole image up to at least the minimum, down to at most the maximum, or both (keeping its aspect). |
| `preresize_min_width` | `INT` | default `1024`, range 0…16384, step 1 | preresize: smallest width the image is scaled up to. |
| `preresize_min_height` | `INT` | default `1024`, range 0…16384, step 1 | preresize: smallest height the image is scaled up to. |
| `preresize_max_width` | `INT` | default `16384`, range 0…16384, step 1 | preresize: largest width the image is scaled down to. |
| `preresize_max_height` | `INT` | default `16384`, range 0…16384, step 1 | preresize: largest height the image is scaled down to. |
| `mask_fill_holes` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | Mark fully-enclosed regions as masked. |
| `mask_expand_pixels` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…16384, step 1 | Dilate mask by this many pixels. |
| `mask_invert` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Invert mask (anything masked is kept). |
| `mask_blend_pixels` | `INT` | default `32`, range 0…64, step 1 | Pixels of feather for stitch blending (lquesada default 32). |
| `mask_hipass_filter` | `FLOAT` | default `0.1`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | Zero out mask values below this threshold. |
| `extend_for_outpainting` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Extend image with edge-replicated padding for outpainting. |
| `extend_up_factor` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.01…100.0, step 0.01 | extend_for_outpainting: grow the image upward by (factor - 1) x its height (1 = none, 1.5 = half the height more). |
| `extend_down_factor` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.01…100.0, step 0.01 | extend_for_outpainting: grow the image downward by (factor - 1) x its height (1 = none, 1.5 = half the height more). |
| `extend_left_factor` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.01…100.0, step 0.01 | extend_for_outpainting: grow the image to the left by (factor - 1) x its width (1 = none, 1.5 = half the width more). |
| `extend_right_factor` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.01…100.0, step 0.01 | extend_for_outpainting: grow the image to the right by (factor - 1) x its width (1 = none, 1.5 = half the width more). |
| `context_from_mask_extend_factor` | `FLOAT` | default `1.2`, range 0.1…100.0, step 0.01 | Resize context bbox around the mask. 1.0 = exact mask bbox. >1 grows outward (1.5 = +50% on every side). <1 shrinks INWARD so the crop is tighter than the mask bbox (0.7 = inset 15% on every side). Useful for 16:9 / portrait single-still crops where the mask bbox is larger than the subject you actually want sampled. |
| `auto_context_factor` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Override context_from_mask_extend_factor automatically so the mask fills ~70% of the resulting crop area. Uses actual mask AREA (not just bbox) to handle sparse / thin / non-convex masks. Result clamped to [0.30, 10.00]. OFF = use the manual slider above. Recommended ON for batch / unattended runs. |
| `aspect_preset` | choice: `Custom`, `Auto`, `Square`, `16:9`, `9:16`, `4:3`, `3:4` | default `"Custom"` | Force the output crop aspect ratio. Custom : use output_target_width / _height as-is (legacy lquesada behaviour - DEFAULT). Auto : pick best of {Square, 16:9, 9:16, 4:3, 3:4} from the mask bbox aspect. Square / 16:9 / 9:16 / 4:3 / 3:4 : keep the SHORTER of (output_target_width, output_target_height) and recompute the longer side from this AR. Multiple-of-padding rounding still applies downstream. |
| `output_resize_to_target_size` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | Force output to a specific resolution for sampling. |
| `output_target_width` | `INT` | default `512`, range 64…16384, step 1 | output_resize_to_target_size: width of the crop handed to the sampler. |
| `output_target_height` | `INT` | default `512`, range 64…16384, step 1 | output_resize_to_target_size: height of the crop handed to the sampler. |
| `output_padding` | choice: `0`, `8`, `16`, `32`, `64`, `128`, `256`, `512` | default `"32"` | Round the crop size up to a multiple of this (models want sizes divisible by 8 / 16 / 32 ...). 0 = no rounding. |
| `device_mode` | choice: `cpu (compatible)`, `gpu (much faster)` | default `"gpu (much faster)"` | Where the resizing and blending run. cpu when the GPU is busy or short of memory. |
| `wan_align_multiple` | `INT` | default `16`, range 1…256, step 1 | Force final crop W/H to multiples of this (Wan VAE patchify; 16 recommended). |
| `wan_temporal_smooth_frames` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…64.0, step 0.1 | Gaussian smoothing of mask along time axis (frames). 0 disables. |
| `wan_stable_crop` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | Use a single union bbox across all frames (Wan replacement-mode). |
| `wan_mask_polarity` | choice: `regenerate_subject`, `preserve_subject` | default `"regenerate_subject"` | regenerate_subject: mask=1 -> regenerate (lquesada). preserve_subject: mask=0 -> regenerate (Wan2.2 replacement: mask=1 keeps environment). |
| `inpaint_mask_mode` | choice: `hard_binary`, `slight_feather`, `soft_blend` | default `"hard_binary"` | What the inpaint sampler sees: hard_binary (crisp), slight_feather (gentle), soft_blend (very soft). |
| `stitch_blend_mode` | choice: `gaussian`, `edge_aware`, `laplacian_pyramid`, `frequency_blend`, `video_stable` | default `"gaussian"` | How the result is composited back: gaussian, edge_aware (Sobel), laplacian_pyramid, frequency_blend, video_stable. |
| `blend_radius` | `INT` | default `32`, range 1…256, step 1 | Feather radius for the stitch blend mask (independent of mask_blend_pixels). |
| `video_stable_temporal_sigma` | `FLOAT` | default `3.0`, range 0.0…10.0, step 0.5 | [video_stable only] Temporal Gaussian sigma in frames. 3.0 ≈ 9-frame window. Higher = smoother but laggier on fast motion. 0 = off. |
| `video_stable_dilate_px` | `INT` | default `-1`, range -1…128, step 1 | [video_stable only] Pixels to push the blend zone into background BEFORE feathering. -1 = derive from blend_radius. 16-32 typical. |
| `video_stable_blur_sigma` | `FLOAT` | default `-1.0`, range -1.0…128.0, step 0.5 | [video_stable only] Spatial Gaussian sigma for the wide feather. -1 = derive from blend_radius (×0.75). Match to dilate value. |
| `fill_masked_area` | choice: `none`, `edge_pad`, `neutral_gray`, `original`, `telea`, `navier_stokes` | default `"none"` | Fill masked region in the cropped image: none, edge_pad (Gaussian smear), neutral_gray, original, telea / navier_stokes (OpenCV inpaint from the surrounding pixels, radius 3 px; only masked pixels change). A pre-fill for low-denoise inpaints. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | The frames to inpaint (a still or a clip). |
| `mask` | `MASK` |  | White = the area to regenerate. |
| `optional_context_mask` | `MASK` |  | Extra area the crop must include as context, without being regenerated. |
| `roto_quality` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Roto-Sync mode: tightens the inpaint seam for clean alpha edges. Forces laplacian_pyramid blend, halves blend_radius (min 4), and pre-erodes the inpaint mask by 1 px so the stitch falls just inside the subject. Safe to leave OFF for general inpaint; turn ON for compositing / roto / VFX work where boundaries must not bleed. |
| `preview_frame` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…100000, step 1 | Which frame the preview under the node shows (0 = the first). Display only: every frame is still cropped exactly as before. |
| `crop_mode` | choice: `pro`, `smart aspect`, `mask prepare` | default `"pro"` | pro: the crop above (lquesada-style, video aware). smart aspect: the stills crop that sizes the output between a minimum and a maximum side (the former Smart Image Crop). mask prepare: no crop - clean the mask and make the inpaint and stitch masks for the whole frame (the former Inpaint Mask Prepare). |
| `smart_resolution_mode` | choice: `Automatic`, `Manual` | default `"Automatic"` | [smart aspect] resolution mode |
| `smart_max_resolution` | `INT` | default `2048`, range 256…16384, step 64 | [smart aspect] Upper limit on the longest side. A crop larger than this is scaled DOWN to fit. |
| `smart_min_resolution` | `INT` | default `768`, range 64…2048, step 64 | [smart aspect] Lower limit on the shortest side. A crop smaller than this is scaled UP, which is the whole point of cropping - a 200px face gets the model's full attention. |
| `smart_manual_width` | `INT` | default `1024`, range 64…8192, step 8 | [smart aspect] manual width |
| `smart_manual_height` | `INT` | default `1024`, range 64…8192, step 8 | [smart aspect] manual height |
| `smart_mask_grow_pixels` | `INT` | default `32`, range -1024…1024, step 8 | [smart aspect] Grow (or shrink) the mask before measuring the crop, so the model sees context around the region rather than a tight cut-out. |
| `smart_patch_mask_holes` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [smart aspect] Close gaps and fill enclosed holes. A subject mask with holes regenerates those holes as background - the hole-in-the-face result. |
| `smart_no_mask_mode` | choice: `Bypass`, `Resize Full Image`, `Crop Full Image` | default `"Bypass"` | [smart aspect] What to do when the mask is empty. Bypass passes the image through untouched, which is the safe default for a batch where only some frames have a mask. |
| `smart_force_divisibility` | choice: `8`, `16`, `32`, `64`, `112`, `128`, `256` | default `128` | [smart aspect] Snap output dimensions to this multiple. A VAE silently pads anything else, which shifts the crop by a few pixels and breaks the stitch. |
| `prepare_fill_holes` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [mask prepare] Fill interior holes in the mask |
| `prepare_remove_small_regions` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [mask prepare] Remove small disconnected blobs |
| `prepare_min_region_area` | `INT` | default `100`, range 0…100000, step 10 | [mask prepare] Minimum region area in pixels to keep |
| `prepare_grow_pixels` | `INT` | default `4`, range 0…256, step 1 | [mask prepare] Dilate mask by this many pixels |
| `prepare_inpaint_edge_mode` | choice: `hard_binary`, `slight_feather` | default `"hard_binary"` | [mask prepare] Edge style for inpaint mask: hard_binary or slight_feather |
| `prepare_stitch_edge_mode` | choice: `gaussian`, `edge_aware` | default `"gaussian"` | [mask prepare] Edge style for stitch blend mask: gaussian or edge_aware |
| `prepare_stitch_feather_radius` | `INT` | default `16`, range 1…128, step 1 | [mask prepare] Feather radius for the stitch blend mask |
| `prepare_temporal_smooth` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | [mask prepare] Apply Gaussian temporal smoothing along batch dimension (for video) |
| `prepare_temporal_sigma` | `FLOAT` | default `1.5`, range 0.1…10.0, step 0.1 | [mask prepare] Temporal Gaussian sigma in frames |
| `region_mode` | choice: `single (as before)`, `per region` | default `"single (as before)"` | single: one crop around the whole mask (as before). per region: every separate part of the mask (8-connected, over the whole clip) gets its own crop and a stable region id; the crops come out region by region (all frames of region 0, then region 1 ...) and Inpaint Stitch Pro (blend) puts every region back. Regions and their boxes are listed in info. mask_invert is applied before the split. |
| `region_min_area` | `INT` | default `64`, range 1…100000000, step 1 | per region: parts of the mask smaller than this many pixels are ignored. |
| `max_regions` | `INT` | default `8`, range 1…64, step 1 | per region: keep at most this many regions (the largest), numbered top to bottom, left to right. |
| `context_detail_aware` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Move each crop edge outward, off detail: when an edge of the context box cuts through more detail (mean luma gradient over the clip) than the image's median, it moves out to the quietest line within context_detail_max_grow. Never inward, so the mask stays inside. The aspect / target step runs after it. |
| `context_detail_max_grow` | `FLOAT` | default `0.25`, range 0.0…2.0, step 0.05 | context_detail_aware: how far an edge may move, as a fraction of the box size (0.25 = a quarter of its width / height per side). |
| `max_crop_megapixels` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…64.0, step 0.05 | Memory / model cap: when output_resize_to_target_size is on and the target size is larger than this many megapixels, it is scaled down (aspect kept) so that, after rounding to output_padding / wan_align_multiple, it fits; never below one multiple or 64 px. The change is noted in info. 0 = no cap (as before). |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `stitcher` | `STITCHER` | Everything Inpaint Stitch Pro needs to put the result back - connect it there. |
| 1 | `cropped_image` | `IMAGE` | The crop to inpaint (send it to your sampler / VAE encode). |
| 2 | `inpaint_mask` | `MASK` | The mask for the sampler, on the crop. |
| 3 | `stitch_blend_mask` | `MASK` | The feathered mask the stitch blends with. |
| 4 | `info` | `STRING` | Sizes, crop box and settings used, as text. |
| 5 | `preview` | `IMAGE` | The picture the node shows: frame preview_frame with the crop window (the crop itself if it cannot be drawn). |


---

### InpaintStitchProMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Inpaint Stitch Pro

Stitch the inpainted crop back into the original (lquesada-compatible) with blend overrides + color match; stitch_mode 'paste back', 'smart' (for a smart aspect crop) or 'context blend' (colour correction and lightness rescue over the original, no stitcher).

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/inpaint_suite.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `blend_mode_override` | choice: `from_crop`, `gaussian`, `edge_aware`, `laplacian_pyramid`, `frequency_blend`, `video_stable` | default `"from_crop"` | Override the blend mode chosen at crop time, or use 'from_crop'. |
| `color_match` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Apply mean+std color transfer before stitching to reduce color shift. |
| `stitch_temporal_sigma` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…10.0, step 0.5 | Post-hoc temporal Gaussian smoothing applied to the per-frame blend mask before compositing. 0 = off. 2-4 = good for jittery segmentation video (≈3 means a 9-frame window). Works on top of any blend mode (gaussian / edge_aware / video_stable / etc.). |
| `stitch_dilate_px` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…128, step 1 | Optional dilation (in pixels) applied to the binary core of the blend mask before temporal smoothing — pushes the seam into flat background. Use with stitch_temporal_sigma > 0 for jittery video. 0 = off. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `stitcher` | `STITCHER` |  | Inpaint Crop Pro's stitcher output (where each crop came from). |
| `inpainted_image` | `IMAGE` |  | The crops after inpainting (same count as the crop node gave; they may be a different size). |
| `roto_quality_override` | choice: `from_crop`, `force_on`, `force_off` | default `"from_crop"` | Roto-Sync at stitch time. 'from_crop' honors the InpaintCropProMEC flag (recommended). 'force_on' applies tight-seam stitching even if crop didn't set it; 'force_off' disables it. |
| `stitch_mode` | choice: `blend`, `paste back`, `smart`, `context blend` | default `"blend"` | blend: the crop's blend mask and blend modes (as before). paste back: resize and paste the crop, optional feathered edge (the former Inpaint Paste Back). smart: the stitch for a crop made in 'smart aspect' mode; needs original_image (the former Smart Image Stitch). context blend: no stitcher - blend a full-frame inpainted image over original_image under mask, with colour correction and lightness rescue (the former Context Inpainter). |
| `original_image` | `IMAGE` |  | [smart / context blend] The original image. |
| `mask` | `MASK` |  | [smart: Mask Feather / context blend] The mask to blend along. |
| `paste_upscale_method` | choice: `lanczos`, `bicubic`, `bilinear`, `nearest`, `area` | default `"bicubic"` | [paste back] Interpolation method for resizing crop back |
| `paste_feather_edges` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | [paste back] Apply Gaussian feather at crop boundary |
| `paste_feather_radius` | `INT` | default `16`, range 0…64, step 1 | [paste back] Feather radius in pixels (only used if feather_edges) |
| `smart_feather_pixels` | `INT` | default `32`, range 0…256, step 1 | [smart] feather pixels |
| `smart_blend_mode` | choice: `Box Feather`, `Mask Feather`, `Hard Paste` | default `"Box Feather"` | [smart] Box Feather softens the crop's rectangular edge. Mask Feather blends along the mask itself, which keeps untouched detail outside the subject. Hard Paste shows the seam and is for checking alignment. |
| `smart_resize_full_image_output` | choice: `Restore Original Size`, `Keep Resized Image` | default `"Restore Original Size"` | [smart] Only applies when the crop node ran in 'Resize Full Image' mode. |
| `smart_enable_color_match` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | [smart] Pull the crop's exposure and cast toward the plate before blending. A feathered seam turns a small mismatch into a visible halo instead of hiding it. |
| `smart_color_match_amount` | `FLOAT` | default `0.35`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [smart] color match amount |
| `context_crop_padding` | `FLOAT` | default `1.2`, range 1.0…2.0, step 0.01 | [context blend] How far around the mask's box (x its size) colour match and lightness rescue look for reference colours when color_reference = around mask. |
| `context_blend_softness` | `FLOAT` | default `8.0`, range 0.0…200.0, step 0.5 | [context blend] Gaussian feather radius applied to the blend mask in pixels. |
| `context_mask_expansion_blend` | `INT` | default `0`, range -100…100, step 1 | [context blend] Per-blend dilate (positive) / erode (negative) of the blend mask in pixels. |
| `context_enable_color_correction` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [context blend] Reinhard mean/std color match between original and inpainted regions. |
| `context_enable_lightness_rescue` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [context blend] Lift CIE LAB L-channel of the inpaint when it is more than ~5% darker. |
| `context_enable_differential_diffusion` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | [context blend] Use \|orig - inpaint\| as a soft preservation weight to keep unchanged pixels. |
| `context_sampling_mask_blur_size` | `INT` | default `21`, range 0…201, step 1 | [context blend] Kernel size (odd) for the additional blur on the output debug mask. |
| `context_sampling_mask_blur_strength` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [context blend] Blend factor for the sampling mask blur applied to debug_mask. |
| `context_color_reference` | choice: `inside mask (same subject)`, `around mask (object removal)` | default `"inside mask (same subject)"` | [context blend] Where colour match / lightness rescue take their target colours. inside mask = the original pixels under the mask (re-rendering the same face or subject). around mask = the ring between the mask and its box grown by crop_padding (removing an object: match the background, not the thing removed). |
| `registration` | choice: `off`, `translation`, `translation + scale` | default `"off"` | [blend] Before compositing, measure how far the generated crop moved against the plate on the context ring (pixels the mask kept) and undo it: translation, or translation + scale (0.94-1.06). Applied only when it improves the match there and stays within registration_max_shift; per-frame results are in info. off = as before. |
| `registration_max_shift` | `INT` | default `16`, range 1…256, step 1 | [blend] registration: the largest shift (pixels, in the crop's size at stitch time) that is accepted; a larger measured shift is ignored. |
| `seam_qc` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | [blend] Measure the seam on every frame: the mean luma difference to the plate along the blend ring (where the blend weight is between 0.02 and 0.98), and how far each frame is from the clip's median. Reported as JSON in info. Changes nothing unless seam_repair_threshold > 0. |
| `seam_repair_threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.005 | [blend] seam_qc: frames whose seam error differs from the median by more than this (0-1 image units) are flagged and repaired on the ring only, from the nearest unflagged frames before / after. 0 = report only. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `image` | `IMAGE` | The full frames with the inpainted crops blended back in. |
| 1 | `blend_mask_used` | `MASK` | The mask the blend actually used, at full-frame size. |
| 2 | `info` | `STRING` | Blend mode and settings used, as text. |

### ProPainterMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** ProPainter — Temporal / Remove / Stitch / Refine / Flow

Unified ProPainter node. Absorbs ProPainterTemporal / Remove / Stitch / StitchRefine / FlowRefine. Pick a mode and only the relevant widgets are read; others are ignored.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/propainter_unified.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | choice: `temporal`, `remove`, `stitch`, `stitch_refine`, `flow` | default `"remove"` | Pick the ProPainter operation. Each mode reads a different subset of the optional inputs/widgets below. |
| `use_half` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | Run the flow-completion and inpainting networks in fp16 on CUDA (about half the VRAM). Ignored on CPU. |
| `color_match_mode` | choice: `off`, `reinhard`, `lab`, `lab_transfer`, `none` | default `"reinhard"` | Per-frame masked colour match between fill and surroundings (ignored in 'flow' mode). |
| `raft_iter` | `INT` | default `12`, range 1…100 | RAFT optical-flow refinement steps. More = more accurate flow, slower. |
| `neighbor_stride` | `INT` | default `5`, range 1…32 | Step between local windows of neighbouring frames that are inpainted together. Smaller = more overlap, smoother, slower. |
| `ref_stride` | `INT` | default `10`, range 1…64 | Spacing of the far-away reference frames each window also looks at. Smaller = more references (fills long occlusions better), more memory. |
| `subvideo_length` | `INT` | default `8`, range 2…300 | Frames per InpaintGenerator window. 8GB cards: 8-30. 12GB: 40-60. 24GB: 80+. |
| `raft_chunk` | `INT` | default `16`, range 1…64 | Frame-pairs per RAFT forward pass. |
| `blend_boundary` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [temporal] Blend the inpaint with original at the crop boundary using stitch_data. |
| `remove_quality` | choice: `fast`, `balanced`, `quality` | default `"balanced"` | [remove] Preset that overrides raft_iter/neighbor_stride/ref_stride/subvideo_length. |
| `remove_dilate_pixels` | `INT` | default `3`, range 0…32 | [remove] Dilate the mask by N px before filling to cover anti-aliasing. |
| `boundary_band_pixels` | `INT` | default `12`, range 0…96 | [stitch] Width of the boundary band re-painted (0 = no boundary repaint). |
| `preserve_inpaint_center` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [stitch] Keep generative inpaint untouched at the centre, only repaint the seam. |
| `upscale_method` | choice: `lanczos`, `bicubic`, `bilinear`, `nearest` | default `"lanczos"` | [stitch] How to scale the inpainted crop to the canvas region. |
| `ring_pixels` | `INT` | default `8`, range 1…64 | [stitch_refine] Half-width of the seam ring in pixels. |
| `flow_consistency_thr` | `FLOAT` | default `1.5`, range 0.0…20.0, step 0.05 | [flow] Forward/backward consistency threshold (pixels). |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` |  | [temporal/remove] Source frames. |
| `masks` | `MASK` |  | [temporal/remove] Region to inpaint. |
| `stitch_data` | `STITCHER,STITCH_DATA` |  | [temporal/stitch/stitch_refine] The stitcher output of Inpaint Crop Pro. |
| `inpainted_image` | `IMAGE` |  | [stitch] Crop-sized generative inpaint output. |
| `stitched_image` | `IMAGE` |  | [stitch_refine] Already-stitched canvas image (output of stitch). |
| `mask_override` | `MASK` |  | [stitch_refine] Optional explicit canvas-space mask (overrides stitch_data). |
| `frame_a` | `IMAGE` |  | [flow] First frame for optical-flow computation. |
| `frame_b` | `IMAGE` |  | [flow] Second frame for optical-flow computation. |
| `flow_mask` | `MASK` |  | [flow] Optional mask restricting the consistency visualisation. |
| `backend` | choice: `propainter`, `auto`, `auto_no_propainter`, `opencv_flow` | default `"propainter"` | [temporal/remove] Which engine fills the hole. propainter: ProPainter only (fails when you have not installed it) - the old behaviour. auto: ProPainter when you installed it (code + weights; S-Lab License 1.0, non-commercial only), otherwise the OpenCV fallback. auto_no_propainter: licence-safe for commercial work, never ProPainter. opencv_flow: model-free OpenCV fallback (optical flow + temporal propagation + Telea) - APPROXIMATE, not ProPainter quality. Nothing is ever downloaded. The info output says which engine ran and why. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `image_out` | `IMAGE` | Primary image output (inpainted / filled / stitched / warped per mode). |
| 1 | `mask_out` | `MASK` | Primary mask output (fill mask / repaint mask / consistency per mode). |
| 2 | `aux_image` | `IMAGE` | QC image: \|output - input\| x4 (flow mode: the flow field as colour). |
| 3 | `aux_mask` | `MASK` | QC mask: pixels this node changed (flow mode: where the flow is unreliable, 1 - consistency). |
| 4 | `info` | `STRING` | Info string with timings, coverage, mode-specific stats. |

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Layer Effects


### LayerEffectsMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Layer Effects (C2C)

Photoshop-style layer effects in one node: drop shadow, outer glow, gradient map, gradient overlay, colour overlay, inner glow, inner shadow and stroke. Switch each on and set its controls; they are applied in the order you list, each on the result of the one before.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/layer_effects/nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** ComfyUI_LayerStyle — chflame163 (https://github.com/chflame163/ComfyUI_LayerStyle) - per CREDITS.md

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `layer_image` | `IMAGE` |  | Foreground layer; its alpha or layer_mask defines where the effects go. |
| `invert_mask` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | Flip mask polarity when your matte is white-on-black. |
| `order` | `STRING` | default `"drop_shadow, outer_glow, gradient_map, gradient_overlay, color_overlay, inner_glow, inner_shadow, stroke"` | The order the switched-on effects are applied in, comma separated; each is applied to the result of the one before. An effect that is on but not listed runs after the listed ones, in the default order. |
| `dissolve_seed` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…2147483647, step 1 | Only used by the 'dissolve' blend mode. Upstream reseeds every frame, so a dissolve flickers through a sequence; a fixed seed here holds still. |
| `drop_shadow` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | Drop shadow: switch it on to show and use its controls. |
| `drop_shadow_blend_mode` | choice: `normal`, `dissolve`, `darken`, `multiply`, `color burn`, `linear burn`, `darker color`, `lighten`, … (+22) |  | Drop shadow |
| `drop_shadow_opacity` | `INT` | default `50`, range 0…100, step 1 | Drop shadow |
| `drop_shadow_distance_x` | `INT` | default `25`, range -9999…9999, step 1 | Drop shadow: Horizontal offset in pixels. Positive moves the shadow right — the opposite of the upstream LayerStyle node, which negated it. |
| `drop_shadow_distance_y` | `INT` | default `25`, range -9999…9999, step 1 | Drop shadow: Vertical offset in pixels. Positive moves the shadow down — the opposite of the upstream LayerStyle node, which negated it. |
| `drop_shadow_grow` | `INT` | default `6`, range -9999…9999, step 1 | Drop shadow: Expand the shadow before blurring. Positive spreads it past the subject edge; negative pulls it inside, which keeps a tight contact shadow from haloing. |
| `drop_shadow_blur` | `INT` | default `18`, range 0…1000, step 1 | Drop shadow: Gaussian blur radius on the shadow matte. |
| `drop_shadow_color` | `STRING` | default `"#000000"` | Drop shadow |
| `outer_glow` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Outer glow: switch it on to show and use its controls. |
| `outer_glow_blend_mode` | choice: `screen`, `linear dodge(add)`, `color dodge`, `lighten`, `dodge`, `hard light`, `linear light`, `normal`, … (+22) |  | Outer glow |
| `outer_glow_opacity` | `INT` | default `100`, range 0…100, step 1 | Outer glow |
| `outer_glow_brightness` | `INT` | default `5`, range 2…20, step 1 | Outer glow: Number of glow passes from core to edge. |
| `outer_glow_range` | `INT` | default `48`, range -9999…9999, step 1 | Outer glow: Initial spread for the outer glow; clamped to frame size. |
| `outer_glow_blur` | `INT` | default `25`, range 0…9999, step 1 | Outer glow |
| `outer_glow_light_color` | `STRING` | default `"#FFBF30"` | Outer glow |
| `outer_glow_color` | `STRING` | default `"#FE0000"` | Outer glow |
| `gradient_map` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Gradient map: switch it on to show and use its controls. |
| `gradient_map_start_color` | `STRING` | default `"#015A52"` | Gradient map |
| `gradient_map_mid_color` | `STRING` | default `"#02AF9F"` | Gradient map |
| `gradient_map_end_color` | `STRING` | default `"#7FFFEC"` | Gradient map |
| `gradient_map_mid_point` | `FLOAT` | default `0.6`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | Gradient map |
| `gradient_map_opacity` | `INT` | default `100`, range 0…100, step 1 | Gradient map |
| `gradient_overlay` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Gradient overlay: switch it on to show and use its controls. |
| `gradient_overlay_blend_mode` | choice: `normal`, `dissolve`, `darken`, `multiply`, `color burn`, `linear burn`, `darker color`, `lighten`, … (+22) |  | Gradient overlay |
| `gradient_overlay_opacity` | `INT` | default `100`, range 0…100, step 1 | Gradient overlay |
| `gradient_overlay_start_color` | `STRING` | default `"#FFBF30"` | Gradient overlay |
| `gradient_overlay_start_alpha` | `INT` | default `255`, range 0…255, step 1 | Gradient overlay |
| `gradient_overlay_end_color` | `STRING` | default `"#FE0000"` | Gradient overlay |
| `gradient_overlay_end_alpha` | `INT` | default `255`, range 0…255, step 1 | Gradient overlay |
| `gradient_overlay_angle` | `INT` | default `0`, range -180…180, step 1 | Gradient overlay |
| `color_overlay` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Colour overlay: switch it on to show and use its controls. |
| `color_overlay_blend_mode` | choice: `normal`, `dissolve`, `darken`, `multiply`, `color burn`, `linear burn`, `darker color`, `lighten`, … (+22) |  | Colour overlay |
| `color_overlay_opacity` | `INT` | default `100`, range 0…100, step 1 | Colour overlay |
| `color_overlay_color` | `STRING` | default `"#FFBF30"` | Colour overlay |
| `inner_glow` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Inner glow: switch it on to show and use its controls. |
| `inner_glow_blend_mode` | choice: `screen`, `linear dodge(add)`, `color dodge`, `lighten`, `dodge`, `hard light`, `linear light`, `normal`, … (+22) |  | Inner glow |
| `inner_glow_opacity` | `INT` | default `100`, range 0…100, step 1 | Inner glow |
| `inner_glow_brightness` | `INT` | default `5`, range 2…20, step 1 | Inner glow: Number of glow passes from core to edge. |
| `inner_glow_range` | `INT` | default `48`, range -9999…9999, step 1 | Inner glow: Initial spread for the outer glow; clamped to frame size. |
| `inner_glow_blur` | `INT` | default `25`, range 0…9999, step 1 | Inner glow |
| `inner_glow_light_color` | `STRING` | default `"#FFBF30"` | Inner glow |
| `inner_glow_color` | `STRING` | default `"#FE0000"` | Inner glow |
| `inner_shadow` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Inner shadow: switch it on to show and use its controls. |
| `inner_shadow_blend_mode` | choice: `normal`, `dissolve`, `darken`, `multiply`, `color burn`, `linear burn`, `darker color`, `lighten`, … (+22) |  | Inner shadow |
| `inner_shadow_opacity` | `INT` | default `50`, range 0…100, step 1 | Inner shadow |
| `inner_shadow_distance_x` | `INT` | default `5`, range -9999…9999, step 1 | Inner shadow: Horizontal offset in pixels. Positive moves the shadow right — the opposite of the upstream LayerStyle node, which negated it. |
| `inner_shadow_distance_y` | `INT` | default `5`, range -9999…9999, step 1 | Inner shadow: Vertical offset in pixels. Positive moves the shadow down — the opposite of the upstream LayerStyle node, which negated it. |
| `inner_shadow_grow` | `INT` | default `2`, range -9999…9999, step 1 | Inner shadow: Expand the shadow before blurring. Positive spreads it past the subject edge; negative pulls it inside, which keeps a tight contact shadow from haloing. |
| `inner_shadow_blur` | `INT` | default `15`, range 0…1000, step 1 | Inner shadow: Gaussian blur radius on the shadow matte. |
| `inner_shadow_color` | `STRING` | default `"#000000"` | Inner shadow |
| `stroke` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Stroke: switch it on to show and use its controls. |
| `stroke_blend_mode` | choice: `normal`, `dissolve`, `darken`, `multiply`, `color burn`, `linear burn`, `darker color`, `lighten`, … (+22) |  | Stroke |
| `stroke_opacity` | `INT` | default `100`, range 0…100, step 1 | Stroke |
| `stroke_grow` | `INT` | default `0`, range -999…999, step 1 | Stroke |
| `stroke_width` | `INT` | default `8`, range 0…999, step 1 | Stroke |
| `stroke_blur` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…100, step 1 | Stroke |
| `stroke_color` | `STRING` | default `"#FF0000"` | Stroke |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `background_image` | `IMAGE` |  | Plate to composite onto; empty = transparent canvas. |
| `layer_mask` | `MASK` |  | Optional matte; overrides the layer's alpha when connected. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `image` | `IMAGE` | The layer with its effects, over the background. |
| 1 | `effect_mask` | `MASK` | Where the effects landed: the union of every switched-on effect's footprint. |
| 2 | `report` | `STRING` | One line per effect applied, with anything worth knowing (clamped sizes, resized masks). |

### BBoxToMask

### MagnificCreationPicker

**Shown in the menu as:** OmniScale Creation Picker

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/magnific/nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** comfyui-mcp — artokun (https://github.com/artokun/comfyui-mcp) - per CREDITS.md

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `project` | choice: `— sign in (Magnific menu) and press R to refresh —` |  | Your Magnific project. Press R after creating one at magnific.com to refresh the list. |
| `folder` | choice: `(project root)` | default `"(project root)"` | Folder inside the selected project; the list narrows to that project. |
| `content_type` | choice: `image`, `video`, `audio` | default `"image"` | Media type to browse; only the matching output is populated. |
| `selected` | `STRING` | default `""` | Set by clicking a thumbnail in the grid. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `image` | `IMAGE` | The picked creation when content_type = image (empty otherwise). |
| 1 | `video` | `VIDEO` | The picked creation when content_type = video. |
| 2 | `audio` | `AUDIO` | The picked creation when content_type = audio. |
| 3 | `creation_identifier` | `STRING` | Its creation id. |
| 4 | `metadata` | `STRING` | Model, prompt, seed, tool and url of the result as JSON - split it with the Metadata (unpack) node. |

### MagnificGenerateImage

**Shown in the menu as:** OmniScale Generate Image

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/magnific/nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** comfyui-mcp — artokun (https://github.com/artokun/comfyui-mcp) - per CREDITS.md

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `prompt` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | What to generate. |
| `model` | choice: `auto` | default `"auto"` | Image model; auto lets Magnific choose. The list comes from your account (press R to refresh). |
| `aspect_ratio` | choice: `auto`, `1:1`, `16:9`, `9:16`, `2:3`, `3:4`, `4:5`, `5:4`, … (+5) | default `"auto"` | Shape of the image; auto = the model's default. |
| `count` | `INT` | default `1`, range 1…8 | How many images; each one is a separate paid generation (above 4 needs confirm_task_count). |
| `seed` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…4294967295 | Sent to Magnific. Same seed and settings reproduce the result on models that honor it; a batch uses seed, seed+1, … per image. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `folder` | `MAGNIFIC_FOLDER` |  | Optional — from a Magnific Save To node. Not connected → your Personal project. |
| `reference_image` | `IMAGE` |  | Optional reference picture. A batch counts one reference per image. Use the other reference_image sockets for pictures of different sizes; reference_type applies to all of them. |
| `reference_image_2` | `IMAGE` |  | Another reference picture (or batch), same reference_type as the first. |
| `reference_image_3` | `IMAGE` |  | Another reference picture (or batch), same reference_type as the first. |
| `reference_image_4` | `IMAGE` |  | Another reference picture (or batch), same reference_type as the first. |
| `reference_type` | choice: `image`, `style` | default `"image"` | How the reference pictures are used: image = content to follow, style = look to copy. |
| `resolution` | choice: `auto` | default `"auto"` | auto = model default; narrows to the selected model. |
| `library_references` | `MAGNIFIC_LIBRARY_REFS` |  | Optional — from a Magnific Library Reference node. Attaches Library characters, styles, elements or locations; their @name tokens are added to the prompt when missing. |
| `confirm_task_count` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…8 | Every image is a separate paid generation. Counts of up to 4 run as they are; for a bigger count type the same number here to submit it. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `images` | `IMAGE` | The generated images as one batch. |
| 1 | `creation_identifiers` | `STRING` | The creation id of each image, one per line. |
| 2 | `metadata` | `STRING` | Model, prompt, seed, tool and url of the result as JSON - split it with the Metadata (unpack) node. |


---

| `duration_seconds` | `INT` | default `30`, range 10…300 | Only Lyria 3 Long and ElevenLabs honor it; the rest are fixed at 30s. |
| `instrumental` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | On: no vocals. |
| `seed` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…4294967295 | Music generation takes no seed: changing it only forces a new run. |

| `folder` | `MAGNIFIC_FOLDER` |  | Optional — from a Magnific Save To node. Not connected → your Personal project. |

### MagnificGenerateVideo

**Shown in the menu as:** OmniScale Generate Video

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/magnific/nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** comfyui-mcp — artokun (https://github.com/artokun/comfyui-mcp) - per CREDITS.md

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `prompt` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | What happens in the video. |
| `model` | choice: `auto` | default `"auto"` | Video model; auto lets Magnific choose. The list comes from your account (press R to refresh). |
| `duration` | choice: `5`, `10` | default `"5"` | Seconds; narrows to the selected model. |
| `resolution` | choice: `auto` | default `"auto"` | auto = model default; narrows to the selected model. |
| `aspect_ratio` | choice: `auto`, `16:9`, `9:16`, `1:1`, `4:3`, `3:4`, `21:9` | default `"auto"` | Shape of the video; auto = the model's default (or the start frame's shape). |
| `sound_effects` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Only models with native audio honor it. |
| `seed` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…4294967295 | Sent to Magnific. Same seed and settings reproduce the result on models that honor it (e.g. Seedance). |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `folder` | `MAGNIFIC_FOLDER` |  | Optional — from a Magnific Save To node. Not connected → your Personal project. |
| `start_frame` | `IMAGE` |  | Image-to-video start keyframe. |
| `end_frame` | `IMAGE` |  | End keyframe (models that support it). |
| `reference_image` | `IMAGE` |  | A picture of what should appear (models that take image references). |
| `reference_video` | `VIDEO` |  | Video reference (models that support it, e.g. Seedance 2.0). Not combinable with a start/end frame. |
| `library_references` | `MAGNIFIC_LIBRARY_REFS` |  | Optional — from a Magnific Library Reference node. Attaches Library characters, styles, elements or locations; their @name tokens are added to the prompt when missing. Video takes characters, elements and styles (no locations). |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `video` | `VIDEO` | The generated clip. |
| 1 | `creation_identifier` | `STRING` | The Magnific creation id of the result (find it in your library, or pass it on). |
| 2 | `metadata` | `STRING` | Model, prompt, seed, tool and url of the result as JSON - split it with the Metadata (unpack) node. |

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |

| `query` | `STRING` | default `""` | What to search the Magnific stock library for. |
| `content_type` | choice: `any`, `photo`, `vector`, `illustration`, `psd`, `design` | default `"photo"` | Kind of stock item; vectors, PSDs and designs come back as JPG renders. |
| `license` | choice: `any`, `free`, `premium` | default `"any"` | Only free items, only premium items, or both. |
| `orientation` | choice: `any`, `portrait`, `landscape`, `square`, `panoramic` | default `"any"` | Shape filter. |
| `ai_generated` | choice: `any`, `only`, `excluded` | default `"any"` | Include AI-generated items, show only them, or leave them out. |

| `range_from` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…999 | Random range start (0 = first result). |

| `seed` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…4294967295 | Drives the random pick: randomize for a different item per run, fix it to reproduce one. |

### MagnificUpscaleImage

**Shown in the menu as:** OmniScale Upscale Image

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/magnific/nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** comfyui-mcp — artokun (https://github.com/artokun/comfyui-mcp) - per CREDITS.md

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | The picture to upscale (uploaded to Magnific). |
| `scale` | choice: `2x`, `4x`, `8x`, `16x` | default `"2x"` | Size multiplier. |
| `precision` | choice: `creative`, `precision` | default `"creative"` | creative = invents new detail (the -10..10 sliders apply); precision = stays faithful to the input (the 0..100 sliders apply). |
| `presets` | choice: `subtle`, `vivid`, `wild` | default `"subtle"` | How strong the added detail is. |
| `engine` | choice: `Automatic`, `Illusio`, `Sharpy`, `Sparkle`, `automatic`, `magnific_illusio`, `magnific_sharpy`, `magnific_sparkle` | default `"Automatic"` | Upscaling engine; Automatic lets Magnific choose. |
| `mode` | choice: `Automatic`, `Magnific v1 (high HDR)`, `Magnific v2 (sublime)`, `Magnific v2 (photo)`, `Magnific v2 (photo denoiser)`, `default`, `ultra`, `ultra-sublime`, … (+2) | default `"Automatic"` | Magnific model version; Automatic lets the server choose. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `folder` | `MAGNIFIC_FOLDER` |  | Optional — from a Magnific Save To node. Not connected → your Personal project. |
| `prompt` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Optional: describe the picture to guide the new detail. |
| `creativity` | `INT` | default `0`, range -10…10 | Creative mode: how much new detail is invented. |
| `hdr` | `INT` | default `0`, range -10…10 | Creative mode: local contrast / definition. |
| `resemblance` | `INT` | default `0`, range -10…10 | Creative mode: how closely the result keeps to the original. |
| `fractality` | `INT` | default `0`, range -10…10 | Creative mode: how much fine, repeating detail the prompt adds per pixel. |
| `sharpness` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…100 | Precision mode: sharpening. |
| `grain` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…100 | Precision mode: added film grain. |
| `ultra_detail` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…100 | Precision mode: extra fine detail. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `image` | `IMAGE` | The upscaled picture. |
| 1 | `creation_identifier` | `STRING` | The Magnific creation id of the result (find it in your library, or pass it on). |
| 2 | `metadata` | `STRING` | Model, prompt, seed, tool and url of the result as JSON - split it with the Metadata (unpack) node. |

| `target_resolution` | choice: `1280`, `1920`, `2560`, `3840` | default `"1920"` | Output width (Magnific/Precision modes). |
| `upscale_factor` | choice: `2`, `4` | default `"2"` | Scale multiplier (Topaz mode). |

| `fps_boost` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | magnific / magnific_precision modes: ask the server to raise the frame rate as well (not sent in topaz mode). |
| `preview` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | 12-frame preview run instead of the full clip. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|

| `flavor` | choice: `natural`, `vivid` | default `"natural"` | magnific mode, custom preset: colour rendering. |
| `premium_quality` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | magnific mode, custom preset: the slower, higher-quality pass. |
| `turbo` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | magnific mode, custom preset: faster processing. |
| `strength` | `INT` | default `50`, range 0…100 | Precision mode only. |

| 1 | `creation_identifier` | `STRING` | The Magnific creation id of the result (find it in your library, or pass it on). |

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Mask


### ImageMaskEditorC2C

**Shown in the menu as:** Image Mask Editor (C2C)

Paint a mask over the image that actually reaches this node. Open the editor to draw; grow, feather, threshold and invert are non-destructive node parameters. mode picks how the mask is drawn: paint (with a colour layer), points + box, shape, spline, roto or video; each mode shows only its own controls. Docs: docs/image-mask-editor.md in this pack.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/image_mask_editor/node.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `editor_id` | `STRING` | default `""` | Auto-set by the editor UI. Don't edit by hand. |
| `frame_mode` | choice: `per_frame`, `shared` | default `"per_frame"` | per_frame: each batch index uses stored frame i; shared: stored frame 0 applies to all. |
| `grow` | `INT` | default `0`, range -256…256, step 1 | Grow (positive) or shrink (negative) the mask by this many pixels. Any non-zero value makes the edge hard first (every partly covered pixel counts); feather softens it again afterwards. |
| `feather` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…256.0, step 1.0 | Soften the mask edge, in whole pixels (0 = off). Gaussian with sigma = 0.33 x this value, the same feather the Smart Crop/Stitch nodes use. |
| `threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | Binarize after feather. 0 = keep soft mask. |
| `invert` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Invert mask after threshold. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | The picture to draw on (B,H,W,3); drives B/H/W. paint and video need it; points, shape and spline take their size from it when connected. |
| `input_mask` | `MASK` |  | Optional mask to combine with the painted mask. points + box and shape draw onto it; video uses it on the frames your keyframes do not fill. |
| `combine` | choice: `replace`, `add`, `subtract`, `intersect` | default `"replace"` | How painted mask combines with input_mask. Without input_mask only replace applies. |
| `mode` | choice: `paint`, `points + box`, `shape`, `spline`, `roto`, `video` | default `"paint"` | How the mask is drawn. paint: brush, shapes, lasso, fill and smart select in the editor (and a colour layer); points + box: clicks and boxes for SAM / SeC; shape: a parametric shape; spline: a spline shape or a pattern along it; roto: Bezier shapes keyed over frames; video: painted keyframes, tweened between. Each mode shows only its own controls. |
| `canvas_layer` | choice: `mask`, `colour` | default `"mask"` | [paint] mask: paint the mask in the editor; colour: paint colour strokes on the canvas (painted_image), their alpha also becomes mask. |
| `canvas_width` | `INT` | default `512`, range 64…4096, step 8 | [paint: colour] Canvas width in pixels. |
| `canvas_height` | `INT` | default `512`, range 64…4096, step 8 | [paint: colour] Canvas height in pixels. |
| `canvas_brush_type` | choice: `paint`, `mask_only` | default `"paint"` | [paint: colour] paint: composite color strokes onto the image. mask_only: build the mask without coloring. |
| `canvas_brush_color` | `STRING` | default `"#000000"` | [paint: colour] Hex color used by the paint brush (ignored in mask_only mode). |
| `canvas_brush_opacity` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [paint: colour] Brush stroke opacity (0 = invisible, 1 = fully opaque). |
| `canvas_brush_hardness` | `FLOAT` | default `0.8`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [paint: colour] Brush profile hardness (0 = soft, 1 = hard edge). |
| `canvas_brush_size` | `INT` | default `20`, range 1…500, step 1 | [paint: colour] Brush diameter in pixels. |
| `canvas_mask_hardness` | `FLOAT` | default `0.5`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [paint: colour] Threshold for the solid inner core: pixels brighter than (1 - hardness) clamp to 1.0. |
| `canvas_mask_expansion` | `INT` | default `0`, range -100…100, step 1 | [paint: colour] Morphological dilate (positive) / erode (negative) in pixels. |
| `canvas_mask_blur_radius` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…100.0, step 0.1 | [paint: colour] Gaussian blur radius applied to the mask edge in pixels. |
| `canvas_mask_blur_strength` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [paint: colour] Blend factor between the hard mask (0) and the fully-blurred mask (1). |
| `canvas_data` | `STRING` | default `""` | [paint: colour] Internal base64 PNG payload from the JS canvas widget. Do not edit manually. |
| `points_width` | `INT` | default `512`, range 1…16384 | [points + box] [draw_*/points_bbox] canvas width |
| `points_height` | `INT` | default `512`, range 1…16384 | [points + box] [draw_*/points_bbox] canvas height |
| `points_editor_data` | `STRING` | default `"{"points":[],"bboxes":[]}"`, multiline | [points + box] JSON from the interactive canvas |
| `points_default_radius` | `FLOAT` | default `3.0`, range 0.5…256.0, step 0.5 | [points + box] default brush radius |
| `points_softness` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…10.0, step 0.1 | [points + box] gaussian sigma multiplier |
| `points_normalize` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [points + box] clamp output to [0,1] |
| `shape_input` | choice: `parameters`, `JSON` | default `"parameters"` | [shape] parameters: the shape from the controls below; JSON: the shapes from shape_json (power mode). |
| `shape_width` | `INT` | default `512`, range 1…16384 | [shape] [draw_*/points_bbox] canvas width |
| `shape_height` | `INT` | default `512`, range 1…16384 | [shape] [draw_*/points_bbox] canvas height |
| `shape_kind` | choice: `circle`, `rectangle`, `ellipse`, `polygon`, `line`, `triangle`, `star`, `diamond`, … (+4) | default `"circle"` | [shape] geometry |
| `shape_cx` | `FLOAT` | default `256.0`, range -16384.0…16384.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_cy` | `FLOAT` | default `256.0`, range -16384.0…16384.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_radius` | `FLOAT` | default `50.0`, range 0.0…8192.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_size_w` | `FLOAT` | default `200.0`, range 0.0…16384.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_size_h` | `FLOAT` | default `100.0`, range 0.0…16384.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_rx` | `FLOAT` | default `100.0`, range 0.0…8192.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_ry` | `FLOAT` | default `50.0`, range 0.0…8192.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_top_left_x` | `FLOAT` | default `100.0`, range -16384.0…16384.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_top_left_y` | `FLOAT` | default `100.0`, range -16384.0…16384.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_x2` | `FLOAT` | default `400.0`, range -16384.0…16384.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_y2` | `FLOAT` | default `400.0`, range -16384.0…16384.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_thickness` | `FLOAT` | default `5.0`, range 0.0…500.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_outer_r` | `FLOAT` | default `100.0`, range 0.0…8192.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_inner_r` | `FLOAT` | default `40.0`, range 0.0…8192.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_num_points` | `INT` | default `5`, range 3…50 | [shape] |
| `shape_corner_radius` | `FLOAT` | default `20.0`, range 0.0…4096.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_cross_size` | `FLOAT` | default `100.0`, range 0.0…8192.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_arrow_length` | `FLOAT` | default `200.0`, range 0.0…16384.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_head_length` | `FLOAT` | default `60.0`, range 0.0…8192.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_head_width` | `FLOAT` | default `80.0`, range 0.0…8192.0, step 0.5 | [shape] |
| `shape_points_json` | `STRING` | default `"[[100,100],[400,100],[400,400],[100,400]]"`, multiline | [shape] polygon vertices when shape=polygon |
| `shape_value` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [shape] fill intensity |
| `shape_rotation` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range -360.0…360.0, step 0.5 | [shape] rotation deg |
| `shape_operation` | choice: `set`, `add`, `subtract`, `max`, `min` | default `"set"` | [shape] blend op |
| `shape_batch_size` | `INT` | default `1`, range 1…256 | [shape] number of frames |
| `shape_json` | `STRING` | default `"{"cx": 256, "cy": 256, "radius": 50}"`, multiline | [shape] raw shape_params JSON (see MaskDrawFrame) |
| `shape_feather` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…128.0, step 0.5 | [shape] [transform/draw] feather radius |
| `spline_data` | `STRING` | default `"[]"` | [spline] Spline payload from the JS canvas. edit/flow_path: single shape list. track: keyframes list [{frame:int, points:[[x,y],…]}, …]. |
| `spline_type` | choice: `catmull_rom`, `bezier`, `polyline`, `b_spline`, `nurbs`, `natural_cubic`, `cardinal` | default `"catmull_rom"` | [spline] interpolation method. catmull_rom/natural_cubic/cardinal pass through the points; b_spline/nurbs are smooth approximating curves; bezier uses tangent handles; polyline = straight. |
| `spline_closed` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [spline] closed loop vs open path |
| `spline_samples_per_segment` | `INT` | default `20`, range 2…128, step 1 | [spline] curve resolution per segment |
| `spline_feather_radius` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…64.0, step 0.5 | [spline] gaussian edge feather |
| `spline_invert` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | [spline] invert mask |
| `spline_smoothing` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [spline] enable spline smoothing |
| `spline_centripetal_alpha` | `FLOAT` | default `0.5`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.05 | [spline] Catmull-Rom alpha (0.5 = centripetal) |
| `spline_width` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…16384, step 1 | [spline] output width (0 = inherit image) |
| `spline_height` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…16384, step 1 | [spline] output height (0 = inherit image) |
| `spline_mask_color` | `STRING` | default `"#ff00ff"` | [spline] preview overlay color (hex) |
| `spline_mask_opacity` | `FLOAT` | default `0.4`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.05 | [spline] preview overlay opacity |
| `spline_fill` | choice: `mask`, `flow pattern` | default `"mask"` | [spline] mask: fill (closed) or stroke (open) the spline; flow pattern: a procedural pattern along it (ribbon, wave, dust, smoke...), animated over frames. |
| `spline_flow_pattern` | choice: `ribbon`, `wave`, `flow`, `dust`, `river`, `smoke`, `sawtooth`, `square`, … (+5) | default `"ribbon"` | [spline: flow pattern] procedural pattern |
| `spline_flow_thickness` | `FLOAT` | default `12.0`, range 0.0…1024.0, step 0.5 | [spline: flow pattern] base stroke thickness |
| `spline_flow_amplitude` | `FLOAT` | default `8.0`, range 0.0…1024.0, step 0.5 | [spline: flow pattern] modulation amplitude |
| `spline_flow_frequency` | `FLOAT` | default `2.0`, range 0.0…64.0, step 0.1 | [spline: flow pattern] modulation frequency |
| `spline_flow_turbulence` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…4.0, step 0.05 | [spline: flow pattern] noise turbulence strength |
| `spline_flow_turbulence_scale` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.01…32.0, step 0.05 | [spline: flow pattern] noise spatial scale |
| `spline_flow_edge_softness` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…32.0, step 0.1 | [spline: flow pattern] edge softness in pixels |
| `spline_flow_taper_start` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [spline: flow pattern] start-end taper amount |
| `spline_flow_taper_end` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [spline: flow pattern] tail-end taper amount |
| `spline_flow_frames` | `INT` | default `1`, range 1…4096, step 1 | [spline: flow pattern] number of animation frames |
| `spline_flow_animation_speed` | `FLOAT` | default `0.05`, range 0.0…4.0, step 0.005 | [spline: flow pattern] phase advance per frame |
| `spline_flow_flow_direction` | choice: `forward`, `reverse`, `bidirectional`, `oscillate` | default `"forward"` | [spline: flow pattern] flow direction |
| `spline_flow_mod_decay` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…4.0, step 0.01 | [spline: flow pattern] modulation falloff over time |
| `spline_flow_seed` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…4294967295, step 1 | [spline: flow pattern] noise seed |
| `spline_flow_use_embedded_editor` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | [spline: flow pattern] show embedded spline preview |
| `roto_json` | `STRING` | default `"{"canvas": {"w": 1024, "h": 1024}, "frames": [{"frame": 0, "splines": []}]}"`, multiline | [roto] |
| `roto_frame_count` | `INT` | default `1`, range 1…4096 | [roto] |
| `roto_width` | `INT` | default `1024`, range 16…8192 | [roto] |
| `roto_height` | `INT` | default `1024`, range 16…8192 | [roto] |
| `roto_samples_per_seg` | `INT` | default `24`, range 2…256 | [roto] |
| `roto_feather_px` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…64.0, step 0.5 | [roto] |
| `roto_frame` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…4096 | [roto] Which keyframe the editor is on. Scrub here, move the shape, and a key is written at this frame. With one key the shape holds for the whole clip; with several the renderer interpolates between them. Does not affect the render on its own. |
| `video_session_id` | `STRING` | default `""` | [video] Auto-set by the editor UI. Don't edit by hand. |
| `video_tween_mode` | choice: `distance_transform`, `linear`, `hold`, `none` | default `"distance_transform"` | [video] Frames BETWEEN two painted frames: distance_transform morphs the shape, linear blends, hold keeps the previous painted mask, none leaves them unpainted (frame by frame). |
| `video_feather` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…32.0, step 0.1 | [video] Gaussian feather radius (px). 0 = none. |
| `video_threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | [video] Binarize after tween. 0 = keep soft mask. |
| `video_outside_keys` | choice: `empty`, `hold` | default `"empty"` | [video] Frames before the first / after the last painted frame: empty (the mask stays on the frames you painted) or hold the nearest painted mask. |
| `layer_output` | `STRING` | default `""` | [paint] Which mask the layer stack gives. Empty: the flattened mask the editor saved (as before). visible: the visible layers re-composited here with their blend modes (add / subtract / intersect), exactly as the editor merges them. A layer name or id: that layer's own pixels only. info lists every layer (mode, visible, locked, metadata, coverage). |
| `spline_keyframes` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | [spline] Animate the drawn spline over frames. JSON list of keys [{"frame": 0, "x": 0, "y": 0, "scale": 1, "rotation": 0}, ...] (x / y in pixels, rotation in degrees) or {"keys": [...], "frames": N, "pivot": [x, y]}. Interpolated between keys, held before the first and after the last; the frame count is the image batch, else frames, else the last key + 1. Fill 'mask' only. Empty = off (as before). |
| `spline_keyframe_easing` | choice: `linear`, `smooth` | default `"linear"` | [spline] spline_keyframes: linear, or smooth (ease in and out between keys). |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `mask` | `MASK` | The final mask [B,H,W] in 0-1: the active mode's mask, combined with input_mask, then grow, feather, threshold and invert. |
| 1 | `preview` | `IMAGE` | The input image with the mask tinted over it (the mask as a grey picture without an image). |
| 2 | `info` | `STRING` | Frame count, stored masks, mode and notes (resized frames, frame recording skipped). |
| 3 | `positive_coords` | `STRING` | points + box: positive points for SAM / SeC; spline: points along the spline. '[]' otherwise. |
| 4 | `negative_coords` | `STRING` | points + box: negative points. '[]' otherwise. |
| 5 | `bboxes` | `BBOX` | points + box: the positive boxes. Empty otherwise. |
| 6 | `neg_bboxes` | `BBOX` | points + box: the negative boxes. Empty otherwise. |
| 7 | `points_json` | `STRING` | points + box: every point as JSON. '[]' otherwise. |
| 8 | `bbox_json` | `STRING` | points + box: the first positive box [x1,y1,x2,y2]; spline: the spline's box. '[]' otherwise. |
| 9 | `primary_bbox` | `BBOX` | points + box: the first positive box [x,y,w,h]; spline: the spline's box. [0,0,0,0] otherwise. |
| 10 | `spline_data_out` | `SPLINE_DATA` | spline: the spline data, for a tracker or another spline node. |
| 11 | `painted_image` | `IMAGE` | paint: the colour layer composited over the image (the image itself without colour strokes). |
