## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Prompt Relay


### PromptRelayAdvancedOptionsC2C

**Shown in the menu as:** Prompt Relay Advanced Options

Optional per-stream tuning for the Prompt Relay encoders.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/prompt_relay/_nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `video_strength` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…10.0, step 0.05 | Multiplier on the temporal penalty. 0 disables segmentation. Most useful in 0–1 to soften boundaries; >1 saturates quickly at the default epsilon — raise epsilon to ~0.1 to make >1 meaningful. |
| `video_window_scale` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…4.0, step 0.05 | Scales the flat anchor zone (default L/2 - 2 frames). <1 starts falloff sooner; >1 widens the rigid zone. |
| `audio_epsilon` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…0.99, step 0.0001 | LTX audio stream epsilon. 0 = inherit from the encoder. |
| `audio_strength` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…10.0, step 0.05 | Multiplier on the temporal penalty. 0 disables segmentation. Most useful in 0–1 to soften boundaries; >1 saturates quickly at the default epsilon — raise epsilon to ~0.1 to make >1 meaningful. |
| `audio_window_scale` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…4.0, step 0.05 | Scales the flat anchor zone (default L/2 - 2 frames). <1 starts falloff sooner; >1 widens the rigid zone. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `profile_action` | choice: `none`, `save`, `load` | default `"none"` | save: store the five values above under profile_name (range-checked) in user/c2c/prompt_relay_profiles.json. load: use the saved profile's values instead of the widgets. none: the widgets as they are. |
| `profile_name` | `STRING` | default `""` | Name of the profile to save or load. |
| `profile_target` | choice: `any`, `wan (native)`, `ltx (native)`, `kijai wan`, `generic` | default `"any"` | The encoder path these options are for: stored with a saved profile and used by capability_report to list which controls that path reads and which it ignores. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `relay_options` | `RELAY_OPTIONS` | Wire to Prompt Relay Encode's relay_options input. |
| 1 | `capability_report` | `STRING` | Which of the five controls the selected encoder path reads and which it ignores (from the code), plus notes on values that have no effect, and the profile used. |

### PromptRelayEncodeC2C

**Shown in the menu as:** Prompt Relay Encode

Unified Prompt Relay encoder (native / smart / kijai). Dynamic sockets via the JS extension. kijai backend: the segment masks are patched INTO the loaded Kijai model (his sampler gives no per-run hook), so they stay after you delete this node - run 'Prompt Relay Restore (Kijai)' on the model, or reload it, to remove them.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/prompt_relay/_nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `backend` | choice: `native`, `smart`, `kijai` | default `"native"` | native = MODEL+CLIP; smart = MODEL+CLIP with auto-segmented prompt; kijai = WanVideoWrapper. |
| `global_prompt` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Persistent prompt across the whole video. |
| `epsilon` | `FLOAT` | default `0.001`, range 1e-06…0.99, step 0.0001 | Temporal penalty decay (sharpness of segment boundaries). |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` |  | native/smart only. |
| `clip` | `CLIP` |  | native/smart only. |
| `latent` | `LATENT` |  | native/smart only — frame count read from shape. |
| `wan_model` | `WANVIDEOMODEL` |  | kijai only — from WanVideoModelLoader. |
| `wan_t5` | `WANTEXTENCODER` |  | kijai only — from LoadWanVideoT5TextEncoder. |
| `local_prompts` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Per-segment prompts separated by '\|' (native/kijai). |
| `segment_lengths` | `STRING` | default `""` | Comma-separated pixel-space frame counts. Empty = equal (native/kijai). |
| `smart_prompt` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | smart only — auto-parsed (`\|` or `Scene N:`). |
| `normalize_by_tokens` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | smart only — scale segment weight by token count. |
| `latent_frames` | `INT` | default `81`, range 1…10000, step 1 | kijai only — (pixel_frames-1)//4 + 1. |
| `negative_prompt` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | kijai only — encoded once for negative_prompt_embeds. |
| `encode_device` | choice: `gpu`, `cpu` | default `"gpu"` | kijai only — device for T5 encode. |
| `relay_options` | `RELAY_OPTIONS` |  | Optional Prompt Relay Advanced Options bundle. |
| `role_sections` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Structured global prompt, one section per line: 'subject: ...', 'action: ...', 'camera: ...', 'environment: ...', 'style: ...'. The sections are added to global_prompt in that order (all backends). Empty = global_prompt only. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `model` | `MODEL` | native / smart: the MODEL with the segment masks patched in - wire it to the sampler. |
| 1 | `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | native / smart: the prompt conditioning for the sampler's positive input. |
| 2 | `wan_model` | `WANVIDEOMODEL` | kijai: the WanVideo model with the segment masks patched in (they stay until Prompt Relay Restore or a reload). |
| 3 | `wan_text_embeds` | `WANVIDEOTEXTEMBEDS` | kijai: text embeds for the WanVideo sampler. |
| 4 | `segment_report` | `STRING` | Every backend: how the text was divided - the full prompt, the global token range and per segment its text, token range, latent / pixel frame range (and weight for smart), as JSON. |

### PromptRelayRestoreKijaiC2C

**Shown in the menu as:** Prompt Relay Restore (Kijai)

Undo a Prompt Relay (Kijai) patch — restores the original cross_attn.forward.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/prompt_relay/_nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `WANVIDEOMODEL` |  | A WanVideo model a Prompt Relay (kijai) encode patched. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `on_foreign_patch` | choice: `restore anyway`, `keep foreign patch`, `error` | default `"restore anyway"` | When another node patched a block's cross-attention AFTER Prompt Relay: restore anyway = put back the original saved by Prompt Relay (drops the other patch, as before). keep foreign patch = leave that block's current patch and only remove Prompt Relay's bookkeeping. error = stop before changing anything. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `model` | `WANVIDEOMODEL` | The same model with its original cross-attention restored. |
| 1 | `patch_state` | `STRING` | Patch state before and after, as JSON: blocks found, patched, foreign patches, restored, anything left behind and a verdict (clean / was not patched / restore incomplete / foreign patch kept). |

### C2C_Submit

**Shown in the menu as:** C2C Farm Submit — Remote Render

Dispatch a full workflow JSON to a remote ComfyUI render-farm backend.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/render_farm.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `prompt_json` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | The workflow to run remotely, in ComfyUI API format (Save (API Format) / the {'node_id': {class_type, inputs}} map). Accepts a bare prompt map or {'prompt': {...}}. |
| `backend` | choice: `local-selftest`, `lan-workstation`, `aks-h100-pool`, `runpod-pod` |  | Registered compute backend (renderfarm/config/backends.json). |
| `compute_profile` | choice: `heavy_94gb`, `light_30gb` |  | Gateway routing profile: heavy_94gb = 1 replica per 94GB GPU, light_30gb = 3 replicas per GPU. |
| `priority` | `INT` | default `5`, range 1…10 | Spool priority — higher dispatches first. |
| `project_name` | `STRING` | default `""` | Audit-log project tag. |
| `wait_for_completion` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | On: block until the remote render finishes and pull outputs back into input/c2c_farm_results/<remote id>/ (the backend's prompt id, not this node's job id; result_paths lists the exact files). Off: return the job id immediately and track it in the C2C Farm dashboard. |
| `timeout_minutes` | `INT` | default `120`, range 1…1440 | How long to wait before the node fails. The job itself keeps running - follow it in the C2C Farm dashboard. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `force_resubmit` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Off: the same workflow on the same backend / profile / project reuses the job already running or finished (no second upload or paid render). On: always spool a new job. |
| `dependency_check` | choice: `off`, `report`, `block` | default `"off"` | Before upload, compare the workflow with the backend's node list: missing node classes, model files / options it does not have, inputs its version of a node does not know and required inputs the workflow leaves out. off: no check (manifest only). report: results in manifest_json, submit anyway. block: stop before anything is uploaded or spooled when something is missing. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `job_id` | `STRING` | The farm job id (look it up in the C2C Farm dashboard or Job History). |
| 1 | `result_paths` | `STRING` | Local paths of the fetched outputs, one per line (empty when not waiting). |
| 2 | `info` | `STRING` | Backend, profile, status and results as JSON. |
| 3 | `manifest_json` | `STRING` | What the workflow needs (node classes, model files) and, with dependency_check on, what the backend lacks, as JSON. |

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/SAM


### SAMMaskGeneratorMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** SAM Mask Generator — Points + BBox + Text

Generate masks using SAM with point + bounding box prompts, iterative refinement, and VRAM offload support. candidate_select can keep the best-scoring or best-measured candidate (quality output); objects_json segments several named objects with their own prompts on chosen frames (object_masks output).

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/sam_mask_generator.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sam_model` | `SAM_MODEL` |  | Loaded SAM model from SAM Model Loader |
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | Input image to segment (first frame is used) |
| `points_json` | `STRING` | default `"[]"`, multiline | JSON array: [{"x":100,"y":200,"label":1}, ...]. label=1=foreground, label=0=background. |
| `bbox_json` | `STRING` | default `""` | Bounding box as JSON: [x1, y1, x2, y2] or {"x":..,"y":..,"w":..,"h":..}. Leave empty to use only point prompts. |
| `text_prompt` | `STRING` | default `""` | Text description of target object (e.g. 'person', 'dog', 'car'). Requires a GroundingDINO model. Converts text to bounding box, then feeds to SAM for precise mask generation. |
| `negative_text_prompt` | `STRING` | default `""` | Text description of objects to EXCLUDE (e.g. 'background', 'wall'). Uses GroundingDINO to detect these regions, then generates negative points from them to suppress unwanted areas in the mask. |
| `grounding_model` | choice: `none`, `groundingdino_swint_ogc`, `groundingdino_swinb_cogcoor` | default `"none"` | GroundingDINO model for text-to-bbox grounding. Set to 'none' to disable text prompting. |
| `text_threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.25`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | GroundingDINO text threshold: how strongly a word of the prompt must match a found box for that word to label it. |
| `text_box_threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.3`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | GroundingDINO box threshold: the minimum confidence for a found box to be kept. |
| `multimask_output` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | Return 3 candidate masks (SAM default) vs 1 |
| `mask_index` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…2 | Which of the 3 candidates to return when multimask=True, in the model's own order (0 = first, NOT necessarily the best score: see the score output; score_threshold keeps the first candidate above it) |
| `score_threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | Discard masks below this confidence score |
| `apply_bbox_crop` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Crop output to bbox region |
| `refine_iterations` | `INT` | default `1`, range 1…5, step 1 | Iterative refinement passes. Each pass feeds the previous mask back into SAM with augmented prompts. 2-3 significantly improves accuracy. |
| `auto_negative_points` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Automatically sample negative points just outside the mask boundary. Helps in cluttered scenes and similar-color backgrounds. |
| `edge_refine` | choice: `none`, `guided`, `guided_strong`, `matte` | default `"guided"` | Edge-perfect refinement of the SAM mask. SAM gives a blocky/binary mask; these snap it to the true object boundary → a soft, roto-grade alpha, robust to motion blur and dull/over-bright colours. none — raw binary SAM mask guided — fast edge-aware guided filter (recommended, no model) guided_strong — wider/softer for hair + fine detail (no model) matte — AI alpha matting (ViTMatte/BiRefNet) for the hardest hair + motion-blur edges; auto-falls back to 'guided' if no matte model installed. |
| `edge_radius` | `INT` | default `8`, range 1…64 | Guided-filter radius (px). Larger = smoother/softer; smaller hugs fine detail. Ignored when edge_refine = none. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bbox` | `BBOX` |  | Bounding box from BBox node (overrides bbox_json) |
| `bboxes_json` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | MULTIPLE boxes to select several regions at once: [[x1,y1,x2,y2], ...]. Each is segmented and unioned into one mask. |
| `spline_json` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Outline the target with a SPLINE and connect/paste its data here (the Image Mask Editor's spline_data_out, spline mode). Its polygon becomes positive points + a bounding box so SAM snaps to what you drew. |
| `existing_mask` | `MASK` |  | Use this mask as the starting point instead of running SAM from scratch |
| `allow_download` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Download the grounding model for text prompts if it is not installed (into its models/ subfolder). Off: an installed copy is used wherever it is; without one a text prompt needs points or a box as well. |
| `candidate_select` | choice: `mask_index (as before)`, `best score`, `best quality` | default `"mask_index (as before)"` | Which SAM candidate to keep, in every refinement pass and at the end. mask_index (as before): mask_index, or with score_threshold the first candidate above it. best score: the candidate with the highest SAM score. best quality: the highest of 0.5 x score + 0.25 x share of its largest piece + 0.25 x how well its outline sits on image edges (a measured heuristic; see the quality output). Both 'best' modes only consider candidates at or above score_threshold. |
| `objects_json` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Several objects, each with its own prompts, optionally on different frames: [{"name": "cup", "frame": 0, "points": [{"x": 120, "y": 80, "label": 1}], "bbox": [x1, y1, x2, y2]}, ...]. Each entry needs points or a bbox; the same name on other frames gives that object frame-specific prompts. When set, the other point / box / spline / text prompts are not used. Each object's frames go to object_masks; frames without a prompt stay empty (no tracking). Empty = off. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `mask` | `MASK` | Selected mask for the chosen mask_index. |
| 1 | `all_masks` | `MASK` | All candidate masks returned by SAM (when multimask_output is True). |
| 2 | `detected_bbox` | `BBOX` | Bounding box derived from the selected mask. |
| 3 | `score` | `FLOAT` | SAM confidence score of the selected mask. |
| 4 | `info` | `STRING` | JSON summary of prompts, scores, and refinement steps. |
| 5 | `object_masks` | `MASK` | objects_json set: one mask per object per frame, object by object (K objects x B frames, object k's frames at k*B..k*B+B-1, in order of first appearance); frames without a prompt for that object are empty. objects_json empty: a copy of mask. |
| 6 | `quality` | `STRING` | JSON: the candidate selection mode, the selected candidate, every candidate's measurements (score, area, pieces, largest piece share, compactness, edge alignment, quality) and the pick of each refinement pass; with objects_json, one such report per entry. |

### SAMModelLoaderMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** SAM Model Loader — SAM2.1 / SAM3

Load SAM 2.1 or SAM 3 model. Auto-detects architecture from filename. Supports VRAM offload.

**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model_name` | choice: `(place model in models/sams/ or models/sam2/)`, `[download] sam2.1_hiera_base_plus.pt`, `[download] sam2.1_hiera_large.pt`, `[download] sam2.1_hiera_small.pt`, `[download] sam2.1_hiera_tiny.pt` |  | SAM checkpoint (.pth/.pt/.safetensors). Models prefixed with [download] will be auto-downloaded from HuggingFace Hub on first use. |
| `model_type` | choice: `auto`, `sam2.1`, `sam3` | default `"auto"` | Model architecture. 'auto' detects from filename. sam2.1: Segment Anything 2.1 (requires sam2 package) sam3: SAM3 (uses SAM2 infrastructure) |
| `device` | choice: `auto`, `cuda`, `cpu` | default `"auto"` | Device to load the model on. 'auto' picks CUDA when available. |
| `offload_to_cpu` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Keep model on CPU between inferences. Saves ~2-4 GB VRAM at cost of slower inference. |

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `allow_download` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Download a '[download]' model if it is not installed (into models/sam2/). Off: installed models are used wherever they are; a missing one gives the model-free stand-in (GrabCut, approximate, labelled by the SAM nodes) and the console names the file to install. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `sam_model` | `SAM_MODEL` | Loaded SAM model wrapper for downstream SAM nodes. |

### SamMultiMaskPickerMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** SAM Multi-Mask Picker — 3 candidates + scores

Run SAM inference to get 3 candidate masks with confidence scores. Pick interactively via the JS widget (click thumbnail or press 1/2/3). Queue the workflow again (Ctrl+Enter) to re-run with new prompts. Works with SAM1, SAM2, and HQ-SAM models.

**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | Input image (B, H, W, C) float32 [0, 1]. First frame used for inference. |
| `model_name` | choice: `sam2.1_hiera_base_plus`, `sam2.1_hiera_large`, `sam2.1_hiera_small`, `sam2.1_hiera_tiny`, `sam2_hiera_base_plus`, `sam2_hiera_large`, `sam2_hiera_small`, `sam2_hiera_tiny`, … (+7) | default `"sam2.1_hiera_base_plus"` | SAM model variant to use for inference. Larger models = better quality, more VRAM. |
| `points_json` | `STRING` | default `"[{"x": 256, "y": 256, "label": 1}]"`, multiline | JSON array of point prompts: [{"x":100,"y":200,"label":1}, ...]. label=1=foreground, label=0=background. |
| `bbox_json` | `STRING` | default `""` | Optional bounding box as JSON: [x1, y1, x2, y2]. Leave empty to use only point prompts. |
| `precision` | choice: `fp32`, `fp16`, `bf16` | default `"fp32"` | Model precision. fp16/bf16 use less VRAM but may reduce quality on some GPUs. |
| `selected_index` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…2, step 1 | Which of the 3 candidate masks to output (0-2). Updated by JS widget on click/keyboard. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sam_model` | `SAM_MODEL` |  | Pre-loaded SAM model from SAM Model Loader node. Overrides model_name if connected. |
| `bbox` | `BBOX` |  | Bounding box from BBox node (overrides bbox_json). |
| `allow_download` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Download model_name if it is not installed (into models/sam2/). Off: an installed copy is used wherever it is; without one the node gives a model-free GrabCut mask (approximate) and names the model to install. |
| `fusion` | choice: `off (selected_index)`, `union`, `intersection`, `weighted by score` | default `"off (selected_index)"` | off: selected_mask is the picked candidate, as before. union: per-pixel max of the candidates in fusion_candidates; intersection: per-pixel min; weighted by score: sum(score x mask) / sum(score) (negative scores count as 0; all 0 = plain mean). The fused mask replaces selected_mask; selected_index and all_masks are unchanged. |
| `fusion_candidates` | `STRING` | default `"0,1,2"` | Comma-separated candidate indices (0, 1, 2) that fusion combines; duplicates are ignored. Read only when fusion is on. |
| `temporal_frames` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…8, step 1 | 0 = off. N > 0 on a clip: the same model and prompts also run on frames 1..N, and each candidate gets a stability score = mean over those frames of its best IoU (masks > 0.5) with any candidate there; listed in info.temporal. A single image only gets a note. |
| `temporal_pick` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | On (with temporal_frames > 0 on a clip): output the most stable candidate (ties -> lower index) instead of selected_index - for the outputs, info and the picker. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `selected_mask` | `MASK` | Mask chosen by the user (or selected_index input). |
| 1 | `all_masks` | `MASK` | Stack of all 3 SAM candidate masks. |
| 2 | `selected_index` | `INT` | Index of the currently picked mask. |
| 3 | `scores` | `STRING` | Comma-separated SAM confidence scores for each candidate. |
| 4 | `info` | `STRING` | JSON summary of prompts, scores, and selection. |

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Sampling


### AsymFlowSamplerPatch

**Shown in the menu as:** AsymFlow Sampler Patch (Lakonik signal-shift)

Replace the model's flow-matching schedule with AsymFlow's shifted-signal-ratio mapping (sigma = r/(shift+r), r=sqrt(t/(1-t))). Inference-only; no retraining required. Best on flow models (SD3, Flux, AsymFlow-trained checkpoints).

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/asymflow_sampler.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` |  | A flow-matching model (SD3, Flux, AsymFlow-trained) whose noise schedule is replaced. |
| `shift` | `FLOAT` | default `3.0`, range 0.05…20.0, step 0.05 | AsymFlow signal-shift. shift=1 -> linear flow. >1 spends more steps at high noise (recommended for high-resolution / Flux-like models). |
| `multiplier` | `INT` | default `1000`, range 1…10000, step 1 | Discretization multiplier (timesteps scale). |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `model` | `MODEL` | The same model with the AsymFlow schedule - wire it to the sampler. |


---

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Segmentation


### LocateAnythingGroundingMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** LocateAnything Grounding (MEC)

Find things in an image by describing them in words - 'the person on the left', 'red car', 'coffee cup' - and get back bounding boxes. Open-vocabulary, so it is not limited to a fixed class list the way a detector is. Feed the boxes to Locate Anything To SAM to turn them into masks. LICENCE: LocateAnything-3B is under the NVIDIA License for NON-COMMERCIAL use (academic and non-profit research only; checked 2026-11-11).

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/locate_anything.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | The picture(s) to search; each frame gets its own boxes. |
| `prompt` | `STRING` | default `"person"`, multiline | What to find, in words: 'red car', 'the person on the left', 'coffee cup'. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model_path` | `STRING` | default `"nvidia/LocateAnything-3B"` | Hugging Face repo id or a local folder holding LocateAnything-3B. |
| `generation_mode` | choice: `hybrid`, `fast`, `slow` | default `"hybrid"` | Passed unchanged to the model's own generate(); hybrid is its default. fast and slow are the model's other modes (see its model card). |
| `max_new_tokens` | `INT` | default `2048`, range 256…8192 | Longest answer the model may write. Raise it when a crowded image returns too few boxes. |
| `device` | choice: `cuda`, `cpu` | default `"cuda"` | Where the 3B model runs. cuda falls back to cpu when no GPU is available (cpu is very slow). |
| `confidence_threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.05 | Has no effect: LocateAnything-3B returns boxes without scores, so there is nothing to threshold. Kept so saved workflows load unchanged. |
| `allow_download` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Download LocateAnything-3B (about 7 GB) into models/LLM/LocateAnything-3B if it is not installed. Off: an installed copy is used (a local folder in model_path, models/*/LocateAnything-3B, or the Hugging Face cache); without one the node names the folder to put it in. There is no model-free way to read a text prompt. LICENCE: NVIDIA License, NON-COMMERCIAL (academic / non-profit research only). |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `bboxes` | `BBOX_LIST` | Every box found, in pixels, with its label and frame - wire to Locate Anything To SAM. |
| 1 | `annotated_image` | `IMAGE` | The image with the boxes and labels drawn on it. |
| 2 | `raw_response` | `STRING` | The model's text answer (frames separated by ---), for checking what it read. |


---

### LocateAnythingToSAMMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** LocateAnything → SAM Prompt (MEC)

Turn bounding boxes into filled rectangles a SAM node can take as box prompts. The rectangle is not the final matte - it tells SAM WHERE to segment, and SAM finds the actual edge inside it. Wire it between Locate Anything Grounding and your masking node.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/locate_anything.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bboxes` | `BBOX_LIST` |  | Boxes from Locate Anything Grounding. |
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | The same image the boxes were found in (sets the mask size and frame count). |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `expand_ratio` | `FLOAT` | default `0.05`, range 0.0…0.5, step 0.01 | Grow each box by this fraction of its width / height on every side, so SAM sees the whole object. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `mask` | `MASK` | Filled rectangles, one mask per frame (a box only marks its own frame) - a box prompt for SAM, not a final matte. |


---

### SegmentC2C

**Shown in the menu as:** Segment (C2C)

Segment (C2C): find objects by words, clicks or boxes with ComfyUI's own SAM 3 / 3.1 and get one mask per object plus their union and boxes. Words: 'person', 'person:3, dog' (word:N for several of one kind). Slice 1 of the unified segmentation node (more targets follow). picks may carry per-frame clicks / boxes ({"frames": {"12": {...}}}): per frame they replace the top-level picks on that frame; when tracking, frames after seed_frame are corrections the tracker restarts from. recover_lost re-finds tracked objects (named by words) where they vanish. face parts / clothes accept groups such as '@lips' or '@head, -hair'.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/segment_c2c.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | A still or a clip (B,H,W,C). |
| `model` | choice: `MISSING: sam3.1_multiplex_fp16.safetensors` |  | SAM 3 / 3.1 checkpoint (ComfyUI's own SAM 3, the same file the Mask + Matting viewer uses; it is loaded once and shared). |
| `target` | choice: `objects`, `face parts`, `clothes` | default `"objects"` | What to segment. objects: things named by words, or picked by clicks and boxes (SAM 3). face parts / clothes: the classes named in the prompt, one mask each (face / clothes parser; model is not used). |
| `prompt` | `STRING` | default `""` | objects: words, e.g. 'person', 'person:3, dog' (with words, clicks and boxes are ignored; empty: use clicks / boxes). face parts: classes such as 'skin, hair, l_eye, mouth' (empty = skin, hair). clothes: 'upper_clothes, pants, dress' (empty = upper_clothes). face parts / clothes also take groups: '@lips', '@eyes', '@head, -hair' ('-' removes), plus your own from groups_json. |
| `picks` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Clicks and boxes in pixels: {"positive": [[x, y]], "negative": [[x, y]], "boxes": [[x0, y0, x1, y1]]}. One object per box (the clicks go with every box), else one object from the clicks. Optional per-frame picks: "frames": {"12": {"positive": [[x, y]], "boxes": [...]}}. per frame (or a still): that frame uses its own picks (not with words). track whole clip: the seed frame's entry finds the objects; a later frame's entry is a correction - the detection there replaces the tracked mask it overlaps most and tracking restarts from it. |
| `max_objects` | `INT` | default `20`, range 1…200 | Keep at most this many objects per frame (strongest first). |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `video` | choice: `track whole clip`, `per frame` | default `"track whole clip"` | A clip: find the objects on seed_frame and follow them through every frame (forward and backward, one tracking state, objects keep their order), or segment each frame on its own. |
| `seed_frame` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…100000 | The frame the objects are found on before tracking (pick a frame where they are clearly visible). |
| `refine` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | EXPERIMENTAL: refine each object's mask once more with SAM, from prompts mined out of the mask itself (SAMRefiner, ICLR 2025). Off by default until it is measured better on your kind of shot; info reports what each pass changed. |
| `allow_download` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | face parts / clothes: allow downloading the parsing model when it is missing. Off: with the C2C setting 'Model-free fallbacks' on (default) an approximate model-free stand-in runs and info says so; with it off the node stops and names the model to install. |
| `recover_lost` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | track whole clip: where a tracked object shrinks below 5 % of its size on seed_frame, look for it again on that frame with its words and put back the detection that overlaps (or sits nearest to) its last good mask. Objects picked by clicks cannot be looked for again (info says so). Ignored with video = per frame. |
| `groups_json` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | face parts / clothes: your own class groups, used as '@name' in prompt: {"my_face": ["@eyes", "nose", "@lips"]}. They win over the built-in groups of the same name. Empty = built-ins only. Not used for objects. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `mask` | `MASK` | Union of every object found, one mask per frame (B,H,W). |
| 1 | `masks` | `MASK` | One mask per object found on the first frame (N,H,W), strongest first. |
| 2 | `bboxes` | `BBOX` | One [x0, y0, x1, y1] box per object of the first frame, in pixels. |
| 3 | `info` | `STRING` | JSON: model, what was asked, objects with scores and boxes per frame, time, and notes. |


---

### SemanticSegmentMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Semantic Segment — Face / Clothes Parsing

Semantic face / clothes parsing using SegFormer.
Select classes by name (comma-separated) to build a combined mask.
Face model: skin, eyes, nose, mouth, hair, hat, glasses, ears.
Clothes model: upper_clothes, pants, dress, shoes, bag, scarf, etc.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/semantic_segment.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | Input image(s) to parse. |
| `model_name` | choice: `segformer_clothes`, `segformer_face` | default `"segformer_clothes"` | segformer_face: 19-class facial parts. segformer_clothes: 18-class apparel. |
| `classes_csv` | `STRING` | default `"skin,hair"` | Comma-separated class names to include in mask. Face: skin, l_brow, r_brow, l_eye, r_eye, eye_g, l_ear, r_ear, ear_r, nose, mouth, u_lip, l_lip, neck, necklace, cloth, hair, hat Clothes: hat, hair, sunglasses, upper_clothes, skirt, pants, dress, belt, left_shoe, right_shoe, face, left_leg, right_leg, left_arm, right_arm, bag, scarf |
| `threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.5`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | Confidence threshold for class assignment. |
| `invert` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Invert the output mask. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `keep_model_loaded` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | Keep model in VRAM between runs. |
| `allow_download` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Download the SegFormer model if it is not installed (into models/segformer/). Off: an installed copy is used wherever it is; without one only 'skin' can be made model-free (by colour, approximate) and the node names the model to install. |
| `groups_json` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Your own class groups, used as '@name' in classes_csv: {"my_face": ["@eyes", "nose", "@lips"]}. Built-in groups - face: @eyes @brows @lips @mouth_area @ears @face_features @head @accessories; clothes: @shoes @legs @arms @body_skin @garments @accessories. A leading '-' in classes_csv removes a class or group ('@head, -hair'). Your groups win over built-ins of the same name. Empty = built-ins only. |
| `temporal_smooth` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…0.95, step 0.05 | Video: smooth each frame's class probability over time before thresholding - a forward and a backward running average across frames (weight of the previous frame = this value), averaged. Fills one-frame dropouts and calms flicker; higher = smoother but slower to follow real changes. 0 = off (each frame on its own). |
| `class_report` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Add a per-frame report to info: the share of the picture each selected class covers (pixels where it is the most likely class) and the frames where a class appears, disappears or changes size by more than 1.5x. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `mask` | `MASK` | Combined binary mask covering all selected semantic classes. |
| 1 | `info` | `STRING` | JSON summary of model, classes used, and per-class pixel counts. |

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Stabilization


### VideoStabilizerMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Video Stabilizer

Video stabilizer: classic feature tracking, RAFT optical flow or OpenCV dense flow, with presets. Wraps the vendored MIT ComfyUI-Video-Stabilizer. Outputs frames + padding mask so it can chain straight into InpaintCropProMEC to fill borders.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/video_stabilizer_mec.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `frames` | `IMAGE` |  | The shaky clip (an IMAGE batch, one frame per image). |
| `method` | choice: `auto`, `classic`, `raft_flow`, `dense_flow` | default `"auto"` | classic = feature tracking (fast; the most accurate in our tests). dense_flow = OpenCV DIS optical flow (no model). raft_flow = RAFT optical flow, experimental: in our tests it beat dense_flow on textured plates and did worst on near-featureless ones. auto = dense_flow on long clips with a free GPU, else classic. |
| `preset` | choice: `handheld_light`, `handheld_heavy`, `vehicle`, `tripod_lock`, `manual` | default `"handheld_light"` | Preset overrides the manual widgets below unless set to 'manual'. |
| `frame_rate` | `FLOAT` | default `16.0` | The clip's frame rate. The smoothing window is set in seconds, so this turns it into frames. |
| `padding_color` | `STRING` | default `"127, 127, 127"` | Colour of the borders the stabilisation uncovers, as 'R, G, B' (0-255). padding_mask marks the same pixels. |
| `framing_mode` | choice: `crop`, `crop_and_pad`, `expand` | default `"crop_and_pad"` | manual preset: crop = zoom in so no border shows (keep_fov limits how far); crop_and_pad = some zoom, the rest padded; expand = a bigger canvas, nothing cropped. |
| `transform_mode` | choice: `translation`, `similarity`, `perspective` | default `"similarity"` | manual preset: which camera motion is removed - translation (shift only), similarity (shift, rotate, zoom), perspective (adds keystone; for heavy shake). |
| `camera_lock` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | manual preset: hold the camera where it is on the first frame (a locked-off tripod look) instead of smoothing its path. |
| `strength` | `FLOAT` | default `0.7`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.05 | manual preset: how much of the shake is removed (0 = none, 1 = follow the smoothed path exactly). |
| `smooth` | `FLOAT` | default `0.5`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.05 | manual preset: smoothing window, 0.19 s at 0 to 0.81 s at 1. Higher removes slower wobble too. |
| `keep_fov` | `FLOAT` | default `0.6`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.05 | manual preset, framing crop: the least of the picture to keep (1 = never zoom in, so frames come back unchanged). |
| `raft_iters` | `INT` | default `12`, range 4…32 | RAFT refinement iterations (num_flow_updates). Used only by method=raft_flow. |
| `use_half` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | fp16 for RAFT on a CUDA GPU (ignored on CPU). Used only by method=raft_flow. |
| `allow_download` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Download the RAFT weights (21 MB large / 4 MB small, torchvision, BSD-3) into models/optical_flow/ when they are missing. Off: an installed copy is used, else dense_flow runs and the info says so. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `motion_meta` | `STRING` |  | Connect the motion_meta output of the Video Stabilizer that made these frames to PUT THE ORIGINAL CAMERA MOTION BACK (after you painted / inpainted the steady plate). The frames must be the stabilised size and count; the other settings are not used then. Pixels a crop framing threw away cannot come back. |
| `horizon_lock` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Keep the horizon as level as on the first frame: the camera's remaining roll (what the smoothing left in) is removed on every frame. Uses the motion the stabiliser measured; borders this uncovers are padded and marked in padding_mask. |
| `horizon_angle` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range -45.0…45.0, step 0.05 | Rotate every stabilised frame by this many degrees (positive = clockwise) - e.g. to level a first frame that was shot tilted. 0 = no rotation. |
| `path_correction` | `STRING` | default `""`, multiline | Your own correction of the stabilised camera path: one keyframe per line, 'frame: dx, dy' or 'frame: dx, dy, degrees' (pixels, positive = right / down / clockwise), e.g. '0: 0, 0' and '48: 12, -4, 0.5'. Values between keyframes are interpolated linearly and held before the first / after the last. A JSON object {"48": [12, -4, 0.5]} works too. Empty = none. |
| `rolling_shutter` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | Rolling-shutter correction: the sensor read-out time as a fraction of one frame (0 = global shutter / off; CMOS phones and DSLRs are often 0.3-0.9). Undoes the skew / wobble fast pans cause, from the measured frame-to-frame motion, in the same warp as the stabilisation. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `stabilized_frames` | `IMAGE` | The stabilised clip (or, with motion_meta connected, the clip with its original camera motion back). |
| 1 | `padding_mask` | `MASK` | 1 where the frame does not cover the canvas: the borders to inpaint. |
| 2 | `info` | `STRING` | What ran: method, flow engine, settings, time, and why a fallback happened. |
| 3 | `motion_meta` | `STRING` | The exact per-frame warps as JSON. Wire it into another Video Stabilizer's motion_meta input to put the camera motion back after editing the steady plate. |


---

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Temporal


### FluidShotDecoderMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Fluid Shot Decoder (Restore Timing)

Selects exactly the frames that correspond to the ORIGINAL input frames using the encoder's TIMEMAP — restores the original frame count and slow-to-fast pacing.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/fluid_shots_audio.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` |  | AI-processed frames (the expanded, normalized batch). |
| `time_map` | `TIMEMAP` |  | From Fluid Shot Encoder. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `allow_approximate` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | When the processed batch has a different frame count than the encoder made (e.g. a model trimmed it to 4n+1), map the original frames proportionally - their timing is then only approximate and the info output says by how much. Off: stop with an error instead. |
| `audio` | `AUDIO` |  | Audio made on the expanded (normalized) timeline, e.g. by an audio-video model. It is retimed onto the original frames with the same map as the pictures (linear between the original frames' positions, speed 1 after the last one), so it stays in sync with the restored clip. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `images` | `IMAGE` | The processed frames at the original count and pacing. |
| 1 | `info` | `STRING` | How the frames were picked; says how approximate the timing is when the count changed. |
| 2 | `audio` | `AUDIO` | The wired audio retimed onto the original frames (nothing when no audio is wired). |
| 3 | `frame_map` | `STRING` | JSON: for every original frame its position on the expanded timeline and the batch frame that was picked. |
| 4 | `integrity` | `STRING` | JSON check of the pick: missing (outside the batch), repeated, out-of-order and identical-content frames, and whether the frame count changed; ok is true only when all are empty. |


---

### FluidShotEncoderMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Fluid Shot Encoder (Temporal Normalizer)

Analyzes optical-flow speed and inserts in-between frames on fast sections so motion per frame is constant — safe for Wan-style AI video. Pair with Fluid Shot Decoder to restore the original timing exactly.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/fluid_shots_audio.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` |  | Video frames [B,H,W,C] 0-1. Speed-ramped source. |
| `max_px_per_frame` | `FLOAT` | default `5.0`, range 0.5…100.0, step 0.5 | Max allowed pixel displacement between consecutive output frames (95th-percentile optical-flow magnitude). Fast sections get flow-warped in-betweens until each step is under this. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scene_cut_threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | 0 = off. Above 0: a pair of frames whose gray histograms differ by at least this (Bhattacharyya distance, 0 = same .. 1 = unrelated; hard cuts are usually above 0.4) is a scene cut and gets no in-betweens, so two shots are never blended. The cut pairs are listed in the TIMEMAP (scene_cuts). |
| `motion_metric` | choice: `global p95`, `local tiles` | default `"global p95"` | global p95: motion = 95th percentile of the whole frame's flow (as before). local tiles: the frame is cut into a 4x4 grid and the fastest tile counts, so a small fast subject (a hand, a ball) on a still background also gets in-betweens. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `normalized_images` | `IMAGE` | The frames with flow-warped in-betweens on fast sections (more frames than the input). |
| 1 | `time_map` | `TIMEMAP` | Where each original frame sits in the new batch - wire to Fluid Shot Decoder. |


---

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Tiling


### IdentityLockMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Identity Lock — refine without moving

Refine every pixel without letting anything move. Keeps the plate's low frequencies - where composition and face geometry live - and takes only the model's high frequencies. The face cannot move, because the only thing taken from the model is the band that does not encode where anything is.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/tiling/_nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `original` | `IMAGE` |  | The plate, at the SAME size as the refined image. If the refinement upscaled, upscale this to match first. |
| `refined` | `IMAGE` |  | What came back from the model. |
| `radius` | `FLOAT` | default `8.0`, range 0.0…128.0, step 0.5 | The split frequency. SMALLER locks harder and lets less refinement through; LARGER passes more detail and more movement with it. If a face is still moving, go SMALLER. |
| `strength` | `FLOAT` | default `1.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.01 | 0 = the raw refinement, 1 = fully locked. Set it to 0 WITH a protect mask to lock only the face and leave the background completely free. |
| `corrections` | `INT` | default `2`, range 0…8 | Extra passes that push the result's structure back onto the plate's. A Gaussian is not a brick-wall filter, so one pass alone holds only about 70% of a face shift; two hold about 94%, at a 2% cost in detail. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `protect` | `MASK` |  | Where the lock is at FULL strength regardless of the strength above - a face. Everywhere else gets whatever strength says. |
| `want_drift_map` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Also output a map of where structure moved. Bright means it moved. Use it to find what the drift number is telling you about. |
| `band_radii` | `STRING` | default `""` | Multi-band lock: comma-separated blur radii in px, fine to coarse (e.g. '2, 8, 32'). They cut the picture into bands: detail finer than the first, one band between each pair, and the structure coarser than the last. Empty = the single split at radius (as before). Needs band_strengths. |
| `band_strengths` | `STRING` | default `""` | Multi-band lock: one number 0-1 per band (radii + 1 of them, fine to coarse) = how much of that band comes from the PLATE (1 = locked, 0 = the refinement's). Example '0, 0.5, 1, 1' keeps the model's fine detail, half the mid band and the plate's structure. strength fades them all; inside protect they apply in full. |
| `track_protect` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | When protect has fewer frames than the clip (e.g. one mask drawn on frame 0), follow the protected region through the original clip (phase correlation, frame to frame) and move the mask with it. The offsets are in drift_report. Off = the mask as given. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `image` | `IMAGE` | The refined detail on the plate's structure. |
| 1 | `drift_map` | `MASK` | Where structure moved (bright = moved); all black unless want_drift_map is on. |
| 2 | `drift` | `FLOAT` | How much structure the refinement moved (lower = closer to the plate). |
| 3 | `info` | `STRING` | Settings used and the drift numbers, as text. |
| 4 | `drift_report` | `STRING` | JSON per frame: structure drift inside the protect region (the whole frame without one), how far that region moved from the original to the result in pixels [dy, dx] (phase correlation), and the tracked mask offset when track_protect is on. |


---

### TileMergeMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Tile Merge

Blend the tiles back into one clip, in float32, with no seam.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/tiling/_nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tiles` | `IMAGE` |  | The tiles after processing, in the order Tile Split gave them (they may be enlarged). |
| `tile_plan` | `TILE_PLAN` |  | The same plan Tile Split used. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scale` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…8.0, step 0.05 | How much the refiner enlarged each tile. 0 means work it out from the tiles themselves, which is right when every tile scaled by the same clean factor. Set it explicitly if the ratio is not a round number. |
| `adaptive_blend` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.05 | Where tiles overlap, give more weight to the tile that has more local detail and agrees better with the blended result (7x7 neighbourhood): weight x (detail / disagreement) ^ this. 0 = the plain cosine / linear blend as before. Pixels only one tile covers never change. |
| `reblend_threshold` | `FLOAT` | default `0.0`, range 0.0…1.0, step 0.005 | Selective repair: overlap pixels where the tiles disagree by more than this (standard deviation of their luma, 0-1 image units, as in seam_heatmap before scaling) are re-blended with the adaptive weights at full strength; the rest is left as it is. 0 = off. seam_report marks the seams it touched. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `image` | `IMAGE` | The clip put back together, seams blended. |
| 1 | `info` | `STRING` | What was merged and at what scale. |
| 2 | `seam_heatmap` | `MASK` | Where the tiles disagree inside their overlaps (weighted standard deviation of their luma), scaled so the clip's worst pixel is 1; black where one tile covers or all tiles agree. |
| 3 | `seam_report` | `STRING` | JSON, one entry per pair of neighbouring tiles: tiles, axis (x = side by side, y = above / below), the overlap band [x, y, w, h] in output pixels, mean and max luma disagreement, and whether reblend_threshold repaired it. |


---

### TilePlanMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Tile Plan

Decide the tiles once for a whole clip. Reusing one plan for every frame is what stops seams crawling.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/tiling/_nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | Any frame at the size you will be tiling. Only its dimensions are read. |
| `mode` | choice: `tile size`, `tile count` | default `"tile size"` | tile size: the largest equal tiles up to tile_size. tile count: a fixed rows x cols grid. |
| `tile_size` | `INT` | default `1024`, range 128…8192, step 64 | A MAXIMUM, not a target. Tiles are equal-sized and must cover the frame exactly, so the size chosen is the largest at or below this that does. The report says what it picked. |
| `rows` | `INT` | default `2`, range 1…16 | tile count: tiles down the frame. |
| `cols` | `INT` | default `2`, range 1…16 | tile count: tiles across the frame. |
| `overlap` | `INT` | default `96`, range 0…512, step 8 | How far neighbouring tiles share pixels. The blend has to hide any difference between two tiles inside this band, so too narrow shows a seam. 64-128px is usual. |
| `blend` | choice: `cosine`, `linear`, `none` | default `"cosine"` | cosine is the right answer: the fade-in and fade-out sum to exactly 1 across the overlap, so there is no seam and no brightness dip. 'none' is for checking where the tiles are. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `vram_budget_mb` | `INT` | default `0`, range -1…1048576, step 256 | Memory budget for ONE tile run of the refiner, in MB. 0 = off (the plan as before). -1 = auto: 80% of the free memory ComfyUI reports for its device. Above 0 = this budget. While the estimate (see mb_per_megapixel) is over it, tile size mode lowers the tile size in 64px steps and tile count mode adds rows / columns. |
| `mb_per_megapixel` | `FLOAT` | default `1500.0`, range 1.0…100000.0, step 50.0 | Your calibration of the refiner: MB it needs per megapixel per frame. The estimate per tile = tile width x height / 1e6 x frames x this (tile-major batches send a tile's whole time range in one run). Only used when vram_budget_mb is not 0. |
| `content_aware` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Move the seams off detail and off the subject: tiles grow by 2 x (overlap / 2, at least 8px) so every seam may shift that far, overlaps never shrink below overlap, and the blend bands go where the image (averaged over ALL frames - one plan per clip) has the least edge detail and subject_mask. Reads the pixels of image. Off = evenly spaced tiles as before. |
| `subject_weight` | `FLOAT` | default `4.0`, range 0.0…100.0, step 0.5 | content_aware: how strongly subject_mask repels a seam, relative to edge detail (0 = ignore the mask). |
| `subject_mask` | `MASK` |  | content_aware: where the subject is (white). Seams avoid it, averaged over the clip's frames. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `tile_plan` | `TILE_PLAN` | The plan: connect it to Tile Split and Tile Merge (the same plan for both). |
| 1 | `info` | `STRING` | What it chose: tile size, grid, overlap. |


---

### TileSplitMEC

**Shown in the menu as:** Tile Split

Split a clip into overlapping tiles, emitted as a batch so any refiner can process them. Wire the result through whatever you like, then Tile Merge.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/nodes/tiling/_nodes.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |  | The clip to cut up (the size the plan was made for). |
| `tile_plan` | `TILE_PLAN` |  | Tile Plan's output. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `halo_px` | `INT` | default `0`, range 0…512, step 8 | Extra context around every tile, in pixels on each side, so the refiner sees what is beyond the tile's edge (frame edges are padded by repeating the border, so every tile keeps one size). Tile Merge cuts it off again - wire THIS node's tile_plan output to Tile Merge. 0 = off. |
| `motion_halo` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | Add the clip's largest frame-to-frame global movement (measured by phase correlation, in pixels, rounded up) to halo_px, so moving content near a tile edge is inside the context in every frame. Needs 2+ frames. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `tiles` | `IMAGE` | Every tile of every frame as one batch, TILE-MAJOR: all frames of tile 1, then all frames of tile 2, in plan order (tile_coords gives each tile's batch_start / batch_end). |
| 1 | `tile_count` | `INT` | How many tiles came out (frames x tiles per frame). |
| 2 | `info` | `STRING` | What was split, as text. |
| 3 | `tile_map` | `IMAGE` | Frame 0 with every tile outlined in its own colour, overlaps darkened and the tile id written in (ids need OpenCV; outlines always). |
| 4 | `tile_coords` | `STRING` | JSON, one entry per tile: id, row, col, x, y, w, h, the four fades, halo, and batch_start / batch_end (end exclusive) - where that tile's frames sit in the tiles batch. |
| 5 | `tile_plan` | `TILE_PLAN` | The plan with this split's halo recorded. Wire it to Tile Merge when halo_px or motion_halo is used (without a halo it is the input plan). |

## 🐺 C2C/🧰 Core/Utils


### FolderIncrementer

**Shown in the menu as:** Folder Version Incrementer

Auto-incrementing per-label / per-date version counter. Scans the output directory for existing `vNNN` subfolders and emits the next one, plus folder name, subfolder path, filename prefix, and full output filename.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/folder_incrementer.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `prefix` | `STRING` | default `"v"` | Prefix before the version number (e.g. 'v' → v001) |
| `padding` | `INT` | default `3`, range 1…10 | Zero-pad width (3 → 001) |
| `suffix` | `STRING` | default `""` | Optional tag like '_mask' or '_wan'. WHERE it lands is controlled by suffix_mode: filename → '.../v001/clip_mask.mov' subfolder → '.../v001/mask/clip.mov' (separate folder per run) folder → 'ATG_..._mask/<date>/v001/clip.mov' (separate top folder) Leave empty to disable. Sanitized for cross-platform safety. |
| `suffix_mode` | choice: `filename`, `subfolder`, `folder` | default `"filename"` | Where the `suffix` is applied: filename — append to the output basename (legacy behaviour) subfolder — nest a folder AFTER the version, e.g. v001/mask/ — keeps mask vs wan outputs of the SAME run side by side, same version folder — append to the TOP folder name, e.g. ATG_..._mask/ — fully separate folder tree with its own versioning. For subfolder/folder a leading '_' or '-' is stripped from the folder name (so '_mask' → 'mask') and the basename is left clean. |
| `label` | `STRING` | default `"default"` | Fallback folder name (used only when no source file is connected) |
| `date_format` | choice: `MM-DD-YYYY`, `DD-MM-YYYY`, `YYYY-MM-DD` | default `"MM-DD-YYYY"` | Date format for the date subfolder (e.g. 02-22-2026 or 2026-02-22) |
| `path_style` | choice: `auto`, `windows`, `linux`, `macos` | default `"auto"` | Path separator style for output strings. auto=detect from current OS, windows=backslash, linux/macos=forward slash. Use 'auto' unless you design workflows on one OS and run on another. |
| `source_choice` | choice: `auto`, `image`, `video`, `exr`, `custom` | default `"auto"` | Where the source name comes from. 'image' → trigger_image, 'video' → trigger_video, 'auto' → prefer video if connected, else image, else legacy `trigger`. 'exr' → prefer an EXR/DPX/CIN plate (sequence or single); works WITHOUT a trigger — wire the plate path STRING into `source_path` and the stem is derived from it. 'custom' → use the ``custom_name`` widget verbatim and ignore all triggers. |
| `name_format` | choice: `basename`, `strip_tags`, `first_segment` | default `"basename"` | How to format the detected filename for folder + prefix: basename — strip extension only (e.g. clip_2160_25fps) strip_tags — also strip trailing res/fps tags (clip) first_segment — keep only the first chunk before . or _ (clip) The original file extension is preserved on output_filename. |
| `numbered_still_mode` | choice: `auto`, `sequence`, `identity` | default `"auto"` | What a trailing number on a STILL means (png/exr/dpx/tif...): auto — >=4 digits is a frame index and is stripped (plate.0001.png -> plate); <=3 digits is a shot number and is KEPT (shot_010.png -> shot_010). sequence — always strip a trailing 2-8 digit number. Use when your frames are bare-numbered (plate_01.png ...). identity — never strip bare digits; only #### and %04d go. #### and %04d are stripped in every mode — they are never part of a real name. auto errs toward KEEPING the number, because over-grouping silently mixes unrelated plates into one sequence, while over-splitting is merely inconvenient and visible. |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `trigger` | `*` |  | Legacy generic trigger – connect any output here. |
| `trigger_image` | `IMAGE` |  | Connect a LoadImage / image source here. Used when source_choice = 'image' or 'auto'. |
| `trigger_video` | `*` |  | Connect a LoadVideo / VHS_LoadVideo / video source here. Used when source_choice = 'video' or 'auto' (preferred). |
| `source_filename` | `STRING` | default `""` | Auto-filled from the connected loader (basename only, no extension). Drives folder name + output basename. Extension is stored separately in source_extension for output_filename. May also be a FULL PATH — the basename is extracted automatically. |
| `source_extension` | `STRING` | default `""` | Auto-filled file extension from the connected loader (e.g. .mp4, .mov). Used only for output_filename — not part of the folder name. |
| `source_path` | `STRING` | default `""` | Full path to a source media file (image / video / EXR / DPX sequence). When provided, the node extracts the filename stem from this path and uses it as the source name — this takes PRECEDENCE over source_filename and custom_name. Connect a STRING output here (e.g. an OCIORead 'source' widget, or any loader that exposes its file path as STRING). The extracted stem is returned on the `source_stem` output, and a trailing version token (e.g. _v001) is incremented to _v002 on the `next_version_token` / `next_version_stem` outputs. |
| `custom_name` | `STRING` | default `""` | Manual source name. Only used when source_choice='custom'. May include an extension (e.g. 'my_shot.mp4'); if no extension is given, output_filename will have none either. Sanitized for cross-platform safety. |
| `base_path` | `STRING` | default `""` | Override base output directory. Leave empty → ComfyUI output dir. |
| `folder_name_override` | `STRING` | default `""` | Force a specific folder name instead of deriving from the input filename. Sanitized for cross-platform safety. |
| `version_group` | `STRING` | default `""` | Which outputs share one version number. LEAVE IT EMPTY (the normal case). Every incrementer in the same run whose folder is the same shot - including its _mask, _exr and _png variants - is handed the SAME version, so one render files as one version. An unrelated job in the same workflow keeps its own counter and is never dragged along. Set a name here only when two outputs belong together but their folder names do not say so. Incrementers sharing a name share a version, whatever their folders. |
| `reserve_version` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | If True, create the version directory and write a `.reserved` marker file to claim the version number atomically. Prevents collisions in batch/render-farm workflows. Leave False for normal use (the directory will be created by ComfyUI's Save node when output is actually written). |
| `layout` | choice: `source / date / version`, `show / shot / task / version` | default `"source / date / version"` | Folder layout. source / date / version - <base>/<source name>/<date>/v### (the usual one) show / shot / task / version - <base>/<show>/<shot>/<task>/v###, the pipeline layout: no date folder, versions always v###, and with reserve_version each version is claimed with an exclusive .lock (retrying when another job took it) plus an optional version_manifest.json. |
| `show` | `STRING` | default `"show"` | show / shot / task layout: show or project name (top folder under base). |
| `shot` | `STRING` | default `"sh010"` | show / shot / task layout: shot identifier (folder under show). |
| `task` | `STRING` | default `"comp"` | show / shot / task layout: task name (folder under shot, e.g. comp, matte). |
| `min_version` | `INT` | default `1`, range 0…999999 | show / shot / task layout: never hand out a version below this. |
| `max_retries` | `INT` | default `5`, range 1…100 | show / shot / task layout, reserve_version on: how many next numbers to try when another job claimed the version first. |
| `write_manifest` | `BOOLEAN` | default `True` | show / shot / task layout, reserve_version on: also write version_manifest.json (workflow hash, user, host, time) into the reserved folder. |
| `versioning` | choice: `folder`, `filename` | default `"folder"` | Where the version goes. folder - a version folder: shot_A/<date>/v001/shot_A.png (the usual one) filename - on the file, no version folder: shot_A/<date>/shot_A_v001.png Filename versioning takes the next number no file (or version folder) of this name has yet, so nothing is overwritten. Save nodes still add their own counter (shot_A_v001_00001_.png); Save Video (C2C) with 'Folder Version' naming does not. |
| `version_in_filename` | `BOOLEAN` | default `False` | folder versioning: also put the version on the file name - shot_A/<date>/v001/shot_A_v001.png. (Filename versioning always does.) |
| `output_name` | `STRING` | default `""` | Optional name for the output FILE only - the folder keeps the source-derived name. e.g. final_render -> shot_A/<date>/v001/final_render.png. May carry an extension (final_render.mov); otherwise the source's extension is kept. Empty = the source name, as before. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `version_string` | `STRING` | Zero-padded version string, e.g. `v001`. |
| 1 | `version_number` | `INT` | Raw integer version number. |
| 2 | `folder_name` | `STRING` | Sanitized folder name derived from the input source (or label). |
| 3 | `subfolder_path` | `STRING` | Full subfolder path: `<base>/<folder>/<date>/<version>`. |
| 4 | `filename_prefix` | `STRING` | Filename prefix combining folder + version (for SaveImage). |
| 5 | `output_filename` | `STRING` | Final output filename including extension. |
| 6 | `source_stem` | `STRING` | Filename stem extracted from the connected source (extension and frame token stripped). e.g. `B_0151C002_260527_134258_a1IE7_v001`. |
| 7 | `current_version_token` | `STRING` | Version token found at the end of the source stem, e.g. `v001`. Empty when the source name has no trailing version token. |
| 8 | `next_version_token` | `STRING` | Source version token incremented by one, e.g. `v002`. Empty when the source name has no trailing version token. |
| 9 | `next_version_stem` | `STRING` | Source stem with its version token incremented, e.g. `B_0151C002_260527_134258_a1IE7_v002`. Empty when the source name has no trailing version token. |
| 10 | `version_path` | `STRING` | Absolute path of the version folder (show / shot / task layout: the folder it allocated, forward slashes unless path_style says otherwise). |
| 11 | `info_json` | `STRING` | JSON: what was allocated - folder, version, paths, whether it was reserved. |

### FolderIncrementerReset

**Shown in the menu as:** Folder Version Check (deprecated) *(deprecated: kept so saved workflows load)*

Report the current version state for a label/date folder. Scans <base>/<label>/<MM-DD-YYYY>/ and returns how many vNNN folders already exist plus the highest version number. To truly 'reset' a label, delete its date subfolder from disk.

**Source:** `ComfyUI-CustomNodePacks/folder_incrementer.py`  
**Provenance:** none named (no PORTED FROM header, no CREDITS / NOTICE section names this file)

**Required inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `label` | `STRING` | default `"default"` | Folder name to inspect |
| `date_format` | choice: `MM-DD-YYYY`, `DD-MM-YYYY`, `YYYY-MM-DD` | default `"MM-DD-YYYY"` | Date format (must match what FolderIncrementer uses) |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `trigger` | `*` |  | Optional any-type trigger input. Connect any upstream output here to force this node to re-run after that node finishes (e.g. wire it to a SaveImage filename to recheck the version state after a save). |
| `base_path` | `STRING` | default `""` | Override base directory. Leave empty → ComfyUI output dir. |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `status` | `STRING` | Human-readable status describing how many versions exist for this label/date. |
| 1 | `current_version` | `INT` | Highest existing version number (0 if none yet). |

| `value` | `INT` | default `1`, range 1…999999 | Create placeholder dirs up to this version number |

**Optional inputs**

| Parameter | Type | Constraints | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|

| `prefix` | `STRING` | default `"v"` | Version-folder prefix. Default 'v' produces v001, v002, ... Must match what FolderIncrementer is using. |
| `padding` | `INT` | default `3`, range 1…10 | Zero-pad width for the version number (3 → v001, 4 → v0001). Must match what FolderIncrementer is using. |

| `date_format` | choice: `MM-DD-YYYY`, `DD-MM-YYYY`, `YYYY-MM-DD` | default `"MM-DD-YYYY"` | Date format (must match what FolderIncrementer uses) |

**Outputs**

| # | Name | Type | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `status` | `STRING` | Status message confirming how many placeholder version dirs were created. |
| 1 | `next_version` | `INT` | The version number the *next* FolderIncrementer run will produce (value+1). |
